    16→// Shadow strategy session state methods.
    17→// Uses session.StateStore for persistence.
    18→
    19→// loadSessionState loads session state using the StateStore.
    20→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) loadSessionState(sessionID string) (*SessionState, error) {
    21→	store, err := s.getStateStore()
    22→	if err != nil {
    23→		return nil, err
    24→	}
    25→	state, err := store.Load(context.Background(), sessionID)
    26→	if err != nil {
    27→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to load session state: %w", err)
    28→	}
    29→	return state, nil
    30→}
    31→
    32→// saveSessionState saves session state using the StateStore.
    33→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) saveSessionState(state *SessionState) error {
    34→	store, err := s.getStateStore()
    35→	if err != nil {
    36→		return err
    37→	}
    38→	if err := store.Save(context.Background(), state); err != nil {
    39→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save session state: %w", err)
    40→	}
    41→	return nil
    42→}
    43→
    44→// clearSessionState clears session state using the StateStore.
    45→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) clearSessionState(sessionID string) error {
    46→	store, err := s.getStateStore()
    47→	if err != nil {
    48→		return err
    49→	}
    50→	if err := store.Clear(context.Background(), sessionID); err != nil {
    51→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to clear session state: %w", err)
    52→	}
    53→	return nil
    54→}
    55→
    56→// listAllSessionStates returns all active session states.
    57→// It filters out orphaned sessions whose shadow branch no longer exists.
    58→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) listAllSessionStates() ([]*SessionState, error) {
    59→	store, err := s.getStateStore()
    60→	if err != nil {
    61→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get state store: %w", err)
    62→	}
    63→
    64→	sessionStates, err := store.List(context.Background())
    65→	if err != nil {
    66→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to list session states: %w", err)
    67→	}
    68→
    69→	if len(sessionStates) == 0 {
    70→		return nil, nil
    71→	}
    72→
    73→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
    74→	if err != nil {
    75→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
    76→	}
    77→
    78→	var states []*SessionState
    79→	for _, sessionState := range sessionStates {
    80→		state := sessionState
    81→
    82→		// Skip and cleanup orphaned sessions whose shadow branch no longer exists.
    83→		// Keep active sessions (shadow branch may not be created yet) and sessions
    84→		// with LastCheckpointID (needed for checkpoint ID reuse on subsequent commits).
    85→		// Clean up everything else: stale pre-state-machine sessions (empty phase),
    86→		// IDLE/ENDED sessions that were never condensed, etc.
    87→		shadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
    88→		refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranch)
    89→		if _, err := repo.Reference(refName, true); err != nil {
    90→			if !state.Phase.IsActive() && state.LastCheckpointID.IsEmpty() {
    91→				//nolint:errcheck,gosec // G104: Cleanup is best-effort, shouldn't fail the list operation
    92→				store.Clear(context.Background(), state.SessionID)
    93→				continue
    94→			}
    95→		}
    96→
    97→		states = append(states, state)
    98→	}
    99→	return states, nil
   100→}
   101→
   102→// findSessionsForWorktree finds all sessions for the given worktree path.
   103→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) findSessionsForWorktree(worktreePath string) ([]*SessionState, error) {
   104→	allStates, err := s.listAllSessionStates()
   105→	if err != nil {
   106→		return nil, err
   107→	}
   108→
   109→	var matching []*SessionState
   110→	for _, state := range allStates {
   111→		if state.WorktreePath == worktreePath {
   112→			matching = append(matching, state)
   113→		}
   114→	}
   115→	return matching, nil
   116→}
   117→
   118→// findSessionsForCommit finds all sessions where base_commit matches the given SHA.
   119→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) findSessionsForCommit(baseCommitSHA string) ([]*SessionState, error) {
   120→	allStates, err := s.listAllSessionStates()
   121→	if err != nil {
   122→		return nil, err
   123→	}
   124→
   125→	var matching []*SessionState
   126→	for _, state := range allStates {
   127→		if state.BaseCommit == baseCommitSHA {
   128→			matching = append(matching, state)
   129→		}
   130→	}
   131→	return matching, nil
   132→}

   135→// Used by the rewind reset command to find sessions to clean up.
   136→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) FindSessionsForCommit(baseCommitSHA string) ([]*SessionState, error) {
   137→	return s.findSessionsForCommit(baseCommitSHA)

   145→
   146→// CountOtherActiveSessionsWithCheckpoints counts how many other active sessions
   147→// from the SAME worktree (different from currentSessionID) have created checkpoints
   148→// on the SAME base commit (current HEAD). This is used to show an informational message
   149→// about concurrent sessions that will be included in the next commit.
   150→// Returns 0, nil if no such sessions exist.
   151→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) CountOtherActiveSessionsWithCheckpoints(currentSessionID string) (int, error) {
   152→	currentWorktree, err := GetWorktreePath()
   153→	if err != nil {
   154→		return 0, err
   155→	}
   156→
   157→	// Get current HEAD to compare with session base commits
   158→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   159→	if err != nil {
   160→		return 0, err
   161→	}
   162→	head, err := repo.Head()
   163→	if err != nil {
   164→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD: %w", err)
   165→	}
   166→	currentHead := head.Hash().String()
   167→
   168→	allStates, err := s.listAllSessionStates()
   169→	if err != nil {
   170→		return 0, err
   171→	}
   172→
   173→	count := 0
   174→	for _, state := range allStates {
   175→		// Only consider sessions from the same worktree with checkpoints
   176→		// AND based on the same commit (current HEAD)
   177→		// Sessions from different base commits are independent and shouldn't be counted
   178→		if state.SessionID != currentSessionID &&
   179→			state.WorktreePath == currentWorktree &&
   180→			state.StepCount > 0 &&
   181→			state.BaseCommit == currentHead {
   182→			count++
   183→		}
   184→	}
   185→	return count, nil
   186→}
   187→
   188→// initializeSession creates a new session state or updates a partial one.
   189→// A partial state may exist if the concurrent session warning was shown.
   190→// agentType is the human-readable name of the agent (e.g., "Claude Code").
   191→// transcriptPath is the path to the live transcript file (for mid-session commit detection).
   192→// userPrompt is the user's prompt text (stored truncated as FirstPrompt for display).
   193→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) initializeSession(repo *git.Repository, sessionID string, agentType agent.AgentType, transcriptPath string, userPrompt string) (*SessionState, error) {
   194→	head, err := repo.Head()
   195→	if err != nil {
   196→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD: %w", err)
   197→	}
   198→
   199→	worktreePath, err := GetWorktreePath()
   200→	if err != nil {
   201→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get worktree path: %w", err)
   202→	}
   203→
   204→	// Get worktree ID for shadow branch naming
   205→	worktreeID, err := paths.GetWorktreeID(worktreePath)
   206→	if err != nil {
   207→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get worktree ID: %w", err)
   208→	}
   209→
   210→	// Capture untracked files at session start to preserve them during rewind
   211→	untrackedFiles, err := collectUntrackedFiles()
   212→	if err != nil {
   213→		// Non-fatal: continue even if we can't collect untracked files
   214→		untrackedFiles = nil
   215→	}
   216→
   217→	now := time.Now()
   218→	state := &SessionState{
   219→		SessionID:             sessionID,
   220→		BaseCommit:            head.Hash().String(),
   221→		WorktreePath:          worktreePath,
   222→		WorktreeID:            worktreeID,
   223→		StartedAt:             now,
   224→		LastInteractionTime:   &now,
   225→		StepCount:             0,
   226→		UntrackedFilesAtStart: untrackedFiles,
   227→		AgentType:             agentType,
   228→		TranscriptPath:        transcriptPath,
   229→		FirstPrompt:           truncatePromptForStorage(userPrompt),
   230→	}
   231→
   232→	if err := s.saveSessionState(state); err != nil {
   233→		return nil, err
   234→	}
   235→
   236→	return state, nil
   237→}
   238→
   239→// getShadowBranchNameForCommit returns the shadow branch name for the given base commit and worktree ID.
   240→// worktreeID should be empty for the main worktree or the internal git worktree name for linked worktrees.
   241→func getShadowBranchNameForCommit(baseCommit, worktreeID string) string {
   242→	return checkpoint.ShadowBranchNameForCommit(baseCommit, worktreeID)
   243→}

    31→	branchMaster = "master"

    34→// errStop is a sentinel error used to break out of git log iteration.
    35→// Shared across strategies that iterate through git commits.
    36→// NOTE: A similar sentinel exists in checkpoint/temporary.go - this is intentional.
    37→// Each package needs its own package-scoped sentinel for git log iteration patterns.
    38→var errStop = errors.New("stop iteration")
    39→
    40→// IsAncestorOf checks if commit is an ancestor of (or equal to) target.
    41→// Returns true if target can reach commit by following parent links.
    42→// Limits search to 1000 commits to avoid excessive traversal.
    43→func IsAncestorOf(repo *git.Repository, commit, target plumbing.Hash) bool {
    44→	if commit == target {
    45→		return true
    46→	}
    47→
    48→	iter, err := repo.Log(&git.LogOptions{From: target})
    49→	if err != nil {
    50→		return false
    51→	}
    52→	defer iter.Close()
    53→
    54→	found := false
    55→	count := 0
    56→	_ = iter.ForEach(func(c *object.Commit) error { //nolint:errcheck // Best-effort search, errors are non-fatal
    57→		count++
    58→		if count > 1000 {
    59→			return errStop
    60→		}
    61→		if c.Hash == commit {
    62→			found = true
    63→			return errStop
    64→		}
    65→		return nil
    66→	})
    67→
    68→	return found
    69→}
    70→
    71→// ListCheckpoints returns all checkpoints from the entire/sessions branch.
    72→// Scans sharded paths: <id[:2]>/<id[2:]>/ directories containing metadata.json.
    73→// Used by both manual-commit and auto-commit strategies.
    74→func ListCheckpoints() ([]CheckpointInfo, error) {
    75→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
    76→	if err != nil {
    77→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
    78→	}
    79→
    80→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName)
    81→	ref, err := repo.Reference(refName, true)
    82→	if err != nil {
    83→		//nolint:nilerr // No sessions branch yet is expected, return empty list
    84→		return []CheckpointInfo{}, nil
    85→	}
    86→
    87→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(ref.Hash())
    88→	if err != nil {
    89→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit object: %w", err)
    90→	}
    91→
    92→	tree, err := commit.Tree()
    93→	if err != nil {
    94→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit tree: %w", err)
    95→	}
    96→
    97→	var checkpoints []CheckpointInfo
    98→
    99→	// Scan sharded structure: <2-char-prefix>/<remaining-id>/metadata.json
   100→	// The tree has 2-character directories (hex buckets)
   101→	for _, bucketEntry := range tree.Entries {
   102→		if bucketEntry.Mode != filemode.Dir {
   103→			continue
   104→		}
   105→		// Bucket should be 2 hex chars
   106→		if len(bucketEntry.Name) != 2 {
   107→			continue
   108→		}
   109→
   110→		bucketTree, treeErr := repo.TreeObject(bucketEntry.Hash)
   111→		if treeErr != nil {
   112→			continue
   113→		}
   114→
   115→		// Each entry in the bucket is the remaining part of the checkpoint ID
   116→		for _, checkpointEntry := range bucketTree.Entries {
   117→			if checkpointEntry.Mode != filemode.Dir {
   118→				continue
   119→			}
   120→
   121→			checkpointTree, cpTreeErr := repo.TreeObject(checkpointEntry.Hash)
   122→			if cpTreeErr != nil {
   123→				continue
   124→			}
   125→
   126→			// Reconstruct checkpoint ID: <bucket><remaining>
   127→			checkpointIDStr := bucketEntry.Name + checkpointEntry.Name
   128→			checkpointID, cpErr := id.NewCheckpointID(checkpointIDStr)
   129→			if cpErr != nil {
   130→				// Skip invalid checkpoint IDs
   131→				continue
   132→			}
   133→
   134→			info := CheckpointInfo{
   135→				CheckpointID: checkpointID,
   136→			}
   137→
   138→			// Get details from metadata file (CheckpointSummary format)
   139→			if metadataFile, fileErr := checkpointTree.File(paths.MetadataFileName); fileErr == nil {
   140→				if content, contentErr := metadataFile.Contents(); contentErr == nil {
   141→					var summary checkpoint.CheckpointSummary
   142→					if json.Unmarshal([]byte(content), &summary) == nil && len(summary.Sessions) > 0 {
   143→						info.CheckpointsCount = summary.CheckpointsCount
   144→						info.FilesTouched = summary.FilesTouched
   145→						info.SessionCount = len(summary.Sessions)
   146→
   147→						// Read session-level metadata for Agent, SessionID, CreatedAt, SessionIDs
   148→						for i, sessionPaths := range summary.Sessions {
   149→							if sessionPaths.Metadata != "" {
   150→								// SessionFilePaths now contains absolute paths with leading "/"
   151→								// Strip the leading "/" for tree.File() which expects paths without leading slash
   152→								sessionMetadataPath := strings.TrimPrefix(sessionPaths.Metadata, "/")
   153→								if sessionFile, sErr := tree.File(sessionMetadataPath); sErr == nil {
   154→									if sessionContent, scErr := sessionFile.Contents(); scErr == nil {
   155→										var sessionMetadata checkpoint.CommittedMetadata
   156→										if json.Unmarshal([]byte(sessionContent), &sessionMetadata) == nil {
   157→											info.SessionIDs = append(info.SessionIDs, sessionMetadata.SessionID)
   158→											// Use first session's metadata for Agent, SessionID, CreatedAt
   159→											if i == 0 {
   160→												info.Agent = sessionMetadata.Agent
   161→												info.SessionID = sessionMetadata.SessionID
   162→												info.CreatedAt = sessionMetadata.CreatedAt
   163→												info.IsTask = sessionMetadata.IsTask
   164→												info.ToolUseID = sessionMetadata.ToolUseID
   165→											}
   166→										}
   167→									}
   168→								}
   169→							}
   170→						}
   171→					}
   172→				}
   173→			}
   174→
   175→			checkpoints = append(checkpoints, info)
   176→		}
   177→	}
   178→
   179→	// Sort by time (most recent first)
   180→	sort.Slice(checkpoints, func(i, j int) bool {
   181→		return checkpoints[i].CreatedAt.After(checkpoints[j].CreatedAt)
   182→	})
   183→
   184→	return checkpoints, nil
   185→}

   197→
   198→// isSpecificAgentType returns true if the agent type is a known, specific value
   199→// (not empty and not the generic "Agent" fallback).
   200→func isSpecificAgentType(t agent.AgentType) bool {
   201→	return t != "" && t != DefaultAgentType
   202→}
   203→
   204→// resolveAgentType picks the best agent type from the context and existing state.
   205→// Priority: existing state (if specific) > context value > default fallback.
   206→func resolveAgentType(ctxAgentType agent.AgentType, state *SessionState) agent.AgentType {
   207→	if state != nil && isSpecificAgentType(state.AgentType) {
   208→		return state.AgentType
   209→	}
   210→	if ctxAgentType != "" {
   211→		return ctxAgentType
   212→	}
   213→	return DefaultAgentType
   214→}

   216→// ensureMetadataBranch creates the orphan entire/sessions branch if it doesn't exist.
   217→// This branch has no parent and starts with an empty tree.
   218→func EnsureMetadataBranch(repo *git.Repository) error {
   219→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName)
   220→
   221→	// Check if branch already exists
   222→	_, err := repo.Reference(refName, true)
   223→	if err == nil {
   224→		// Branch already exists
   225→		return nil
   226→	}
   227→
   228→	// Create empty tree (no files)
   229→	emptyTree := &object.Tree{Entries: []object.TreeEntry{}}
   230→	obj := repo.Storer.NewEncodedObject()
   231→	if err := emptyTree.Encode(obj); err != nil {
   232→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to encode empty tree: %w", err)
   233→	}
   234→	emptyTreeHash, err := repo.Storer.SetEncodedObject(obj)
   235→	if err != nil {
   236→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to store empty tree: %w", err)
   237→	}
   238→
   239→	// Create orphan commit (no parent)
   240→	now := time.Now()
   241→	sig := object.Signature{
   242→		Name:  "Entire",
   243→		Email: "entire@local",
   244→		When:  now,
   245→	}
   246→
   247→	commit := &object.Commit{
   248→		TreeHash:  emptyTreeHash,
   249→		Author:    sig,
   250→		Committer: sig,
   251→		Message:   "Initialize metadata branch\n\nThis branch stores session metadata for the auto-commit strategy.\n",
   252→	}
   253→	// Note: No ParentHashes - this is an orphan commit
   254→
   255→	commitObj := repo.Storer.NewEncodedObject()
   256→	if err := commit.Encode(commitObj); err != nil {
   257→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to encode orphan commit: %w", err)
   258→	}
   259→	commitHash, err := repo.Storer.SetEncodedObject(commitObj)
   260→	if err != nil {
   261→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to store orphan commit: %w", err)
   262→	}
   263→
   264→	// Create branch reference
   265→	ref := plumbing.NewHashReference(refName, commitHash)
   266→	if err := repo.Storer.SetReference(ref); err != nil {
   267→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create metadata branch: %w", err)
   268→	}
   269→
   270→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "✓ Created orphan branch '%s' for session metadata\n", paths.MetadataBranchName)
   271→	return nil
   272→}
   273→
   274→// readCheckpointMetadata reads metadata.json from a checkpoint path on entire/sessions.
   275→// With the new format, root metadata.json is a CheckpointSummary with Agents array.
   276→// This function reads the summary and extracts relevant fields into CheckpointInfo,
   277→// also reading session-level metadata for IsTask/ToolUseID fields.
   278→func ReadCheckpointMetadata(tree *object.Tree, checkpointPath string) (*CheckpointInfo, error) {
   279→	metadataPath := checkpointPath + "/metadata.json"
   280→	file, err := tree.File(metadataPath)
   281→	if err != nil {
   282→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to find metadata at %s: %w", metadataPath, err)
   283→	}
   284→
   285→	content, err := file.Contents()
   286→	if err != nil {
   287→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read metadata: %w", err)
   288→	}
   289→
   290→	// Try to parse as CheckpointSummary first (new format)
   291→	var summary checkpoint.CheckpointSummary
   292→	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(content), &summary); err == nil {
   293→		// If we have sessions array, this is the new format
   294→		if len(summary.Sessions) > 0 {
   295→			info := &CheckpointInfo{
   296→				CheckpointID:     summary.CheckpointID,
   297→				CheckpointsCount: summary.CheckpointsCount,
   298→				FilesTouched:     summary.FilesTouched,
   299→				SessionCount:     len(summary.Sessions),
   300→			}
   301→
   302→			// Read all sessions' metadata to populate SessionIDs and get other fields from first session
   303→			var sessionIDs []string
   304→			for i, sessionPaths := range summary.Sessions {
   305→				if sessionPaths.Metadata != "" {
   306→					// SessionFilePaths now contains absolute paths with leading "/"
   307→					// Strip the leading "/" for tree.File() which expects paths without leading slash
   308→					sessionMetadataPath := strings.TrimPrefix(sessionPaths.Metadata, "/")
   309→					if sessionFile, err := tree.File(sessionMetadataPath); err == nil {
   310→						if sessionContent, err := sessionFile.Contents(); err == nil {
   311→							var sessionMetadata checkpoint.CommittedMetadata
   312→							if json.Unmarshal([]byte(sessionContent), &sessionMetadata) == nil {
   313→								sessionIDs = append(sessionIDs, sessionMetadata.SessionID)
   314→								// Use first session for Agent, SessionID, CreatedAt, IsTask, ToolUseID
   315→								if i == 0 {
   316→									info.Agent = sessionMetadata.Agent
   317→									info.SessionID = sessionMetadata.SessionID
   318→									info.CreatedAt = sessionMetadata.CreatedAt
   319→									info.IsTask = sessionMetadata.IsTask
   320→									info.ToolUseID = sessionMetadata.ToolUseID
   321→								}
   322→							}
   323→						}
   324→					}
   325→				}
   326→			}
   327→			info.SessionIDs = sessionIDs
   328→
   329→			return info, nil
   330→		}
   331→	}
   332→
   333→	// Fall back to parsing as CheckpointInfo (old format or direct info)
   334→	var metadata CheckpointInfo
   335→	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(content), &metadata); err != nil {
   336→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse metadata: %w", err)
   337→	}
   338→
   339→	return &metadata, nil
   340→}

   342→// GetMetadataBranchTree returns the tree object for the entire/sessions branch.

   345→	ref, err := repo.Reference(refName, true)

   348→	}
   349→
   350→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(ref.Hash())
   351→	if err != nil {

   353→	}
   354→
   355→	tree, err := commit.Tree()

   361→
   362→// ExtractFirstPrompt extracts and truncates the first meaningful prompt from prompt content.
   363→// Prompts are separated by "\n\n---\n\n". Skips empty prompts and separator-only content.
   364→// Returns empty string if no valid prompt is found.
   365→func ExtractFirstPrompt(content string) string {
   366→	if content == "" {
   367→		return ""
   368→	}
   369→
   370→	// Prompts are separated by "\n\n---\n\n"
   371→	// Find the first non-empty prompt
   372→	prompts := strings.Split(content, "\n\n---\n\n")
   373→	var firstPrompt string
   374→	for _, p := range prompts {
   375→		cleaned := strings.TrimSpace(p)
   376→		// Skip empty prompts or prompts that are just dashes/separators
   377→		if cleaned == "" || isOnlySeparators(cleaned) {
   378→			continue
   379→		}
   380→		firstPrompt = cleaned
   381→		break
   382→	}
   383→
   384→	if firstPrompt == "" {
   385→		return ""
   386→	}
   387→
   388→	return TruncateDescription(firstPrompt, MaxDescriptionLength)
   389→}
   390→
   391→// ReadSessionPromptFromTree reads the first meaningful prompt from a checkpoint's prompt.txt file in a git tree.
   392→// Returns an empty string if the prompt cannot be read.
   393→func ReadSessionPromptFromTree(tree *object.Tree, checkpointPath string) string {
   394→	promptPath := checkpointPath + "/" + paths.PromptFileName
   395→	file, err := tree.File(promptPath)
   396→	if err != nil {
   397→		return ""
   398→	}
   399→
   400→	content, err := file.Contents()
   401→	if err != nil {
   402→		return ""
   403→	}
   404→
   405→	return ExtractFirstPrompt(content)
   406→}
   407→
   408→// isOnlySeparators checks if a string contains only dashes, spaces, and newlines.
   409→func isOnlySeparators(s string) bool {
   410→	for _, r := range s {
   411→		if r != '-' && r != ' ' && r != '\n' && r != '\r' && r != '\t' {
   412→			return false
   413→		}
   414→	}
   415→	return true
   416→}
   417→
   418→// ReadAllSessionPromptsFromTree reads the first prompt for all sessions in a multi-session checkpoint.
   419→// Returns a slice of prompts parallel to sessionIDs (oldest to newest).
   420→// For single-session checkpoints, returns a slice with just the root prompt.
   421→func ReadAllSessionPromptsFromTree(tree *object.Tree, checkpointPath string, sessionCount int, sessionIDs []string) []string {
   422→	if sessionCount <= 1 || len(sessionIDs) <= 1 {
   423→		// Single session - just return the root prompt
   424→		prompt := ReadSessionPromptFromTree(tree, checkpointPath)
   425→		if prompt != "" {
   426→			return []string{prompt}
   427→		}
   428→		return nil
   429→	}
   430→
   431→	// Multi-session: read prompts from archived folders (0/, 1/, etc.) and root
   432→	prompts := make([]string, len(sessionIDs))
   433→
   434→	// Read archived session prompts (folders 0, 1, ... N-2)
   435→	for i := range sessionCount - 1 {
   436→		archivedPath := fmt.Sprintf("%s/%d", checkpointPath, i)
   437→		prompts[i] = ReadSessionPromptFromTree(tree, archivedPath)
   438→	}
   439→
   440→	// Read the most recent session prompt (at root level)
   441→	prompts[len(prompts)-1] = ReadSessionPromptFromTree(tree, checkpointPath)
   442→
   443→	return prompts
   444→}
   445→
   446→// ReadSessionPromptFromShadow reads the first prompt for a session from the shadow branch.
   447→// Returns an empty string if the prompt cannot be read.
   448→func ReadSessionPromptFromShadow(repo *git.Repository, baseCommit, worktreeID, sessionID string) string {
   449→	// Get shadow branch for this base commit using worktree-specific naming
   450→	shadowBranchName := checkpoint.ShadowBranchNameForCommit(baseCommit, worktreeID)
   451→	ref, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranchName), true)
   452→	if err != nil {
   453→		return ""
   454→	}
   455→
   456→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(ref.Hash())
   457→	if err != nil {
   458→		return ""
   459→	}
   460→
   461→	tree, err := commit.Tree()
   462→	if err != nil {
   463→		return ""
   464→	}
   465→
   466→	// Build the path to prompt.txt: .entire/metadata/<session-id>/prompt.txt
   467→	checkpointPath := paths.EntireMetadataDir + "/" + sessionID
   468→	return ReadSessionPromptFromTree(tree, checkpointPath)
   469→}

   471→// GetRemoteMetadataBranchTree returns the tree object for origin/entire/sessions.
   472→func GetRemoteMetadataBranchTree(repo *git.Repository) (*object.Tree, error) {
   473→	refName := plumbing.NewRemoteReferenceName("origin", paths.MetadataBranchName)
   474→	ref, err := repo.Reference(refName, true)
   475→	if err != nil {
   476→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get remote metadata branch reference: %w", err)
   477→	}
   478→
   479→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(ref.Hash())
   480→	if err != nil {
   481→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get remote metadata branch commit: %w", err)
   482→	}
   483→
   484→	tree, err := commit.Tree()
   485→	if err != nil {
   486→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get remote metadata branch tree: %w", err)
   487→	}
   488→	return tree, nil
   489→}
   490→
   491→// OpenRepository opens the git repository with linked worktree support enabled.
   492→// It uses git.PlainOpenWithOptions with EnableDotGitCommonDir set to true,
   493→// which is required for proper operation in git worktrees created via 'git worktree add'.
   494→//
   495→// Without EnableDotGitCommonDir, go-git operations in worktrees can silently fail:
   496→// - Commits appear to succeed but are not persisted
   497→// - Refs are written to incorrect locations
   498→// - The worktree's HEAD/index don't get updated properly
   499→//
   500→// This happens because worktrees use .git as a file (pointing to the main repo)
   501→// rather than a directory, and go-git needs to route paths correctly between
   502→// shared (.git/) and per-worktree (.git/worktrees/<name>/) locations.
   503→//
   504→// The function first uses 'git rev-parse --show-toplevel' to find the repository
   505→// root, which works correctly even when called from a subdirectory within the repo.
   506→func OpenRepository() (*git.Repository, error) {
   507→	// First, find the repository root using git rev-parse --show-toplevel
   508→	// This works correctly from any subdirectory within the repository
   509→	repoRoot, err := GetWorktreePath()
   510→	if err != nil {
   511→		// Fallback to current directory if git command fails
   512→		// (e.g., if git is not installed or we're not in a repo)
   513→		repoRoot = "."
   514→	}
   515→
   516→	repo, err := git.PlainOpenWithOptions(repoRoot, &git.PlainOpenOptions{
   517→		EnableDotGitCommonDir: true,
   518→	})
   519→	if err != nil {
   520→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to open repository: %w", err)
   521→	}
   522→	return repo, nil
   523→}
   524→
   525→// IsInsideWorktree returns true if the current directory is inside a git worktree
   526→// (as opposed to the main repository). Worktrees have .git as a file pointing
   527→// to the main repo, while the main repo has .git as a directory.
   528→// This function works correctly from any subdirectory within the repository.
   529→func IsInsideWorktree() bool {
   530→	// First find the repository root
   531→	repoRoot, err := GetWorktreePath()
   532→	if err != nil {
   533→		return false
   534→	}
   535→
   536→	gitPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, gitDir)
   537→	gitInfo, err := os.Stat(gitPath)
   538→	if err != nil {
   539→		return false
   540→	}
   541→	return !gitInfo.IsDir()
   542→}
   543→
   544→// GetMainRepoRoot returns the root directory of the main repository.
   545→// In the main repo, this is the worktree path (repo root).
   546→// In a worktree, this parses the .git file to find the main repo.
   547→// This function works correctly from any subdirectory within the repository.
   548→//
   549→// Per gitrepository-layout(5), a worktree's .git file is a "gitfile" containing
   550→// "gitdir: <path>" pointing to $GIT_DIR/worktrees/<id> in the main repository.
   551→// See: https://git-scm.com/docs/gitrepository-layout
   552→func GetMainRepoRoot() (string, error) {
   553→	// First find the worktree/repo root
   554→	repoRoot, err := GetWorktreePath()
   555→	if err != nil {
   556→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get worktree path: %w", err)
   557→	}
   558→
   559→	if !IsInsideWorktree() {
   560→		return repoRoot, nil
   561→	}
   562→
   563→	// Worktree .git file contains: "gitdir: /path/to/main/.git/worktrees/<id>"
   564→	gitFilePath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, gitDir)
   565→	content, err := os.ReadFile(gitFilePath) //nolint:gosec // G304: gitFilePath is constructed from repo root, not user input
   566→	if err != nil {
   567→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to read .git file: %w", err)
   568→	}
   569→
   570→	gitdir := strings.TrimSpace(string(content))
   571→	gitdir = strings.TrimPrefix(gitdir, "gitdir: ")
   572→
   573→	// Extract main repo root: everything before "/.git/"
   574→	idx := strings.LastIndex(gitdir, "/.git/")
   575→	if idx < 0 {
   576→		return "", fmt.Errorf("unexpected gitdir format: %s", gitdir)
   577→	}
   578→	return gitdir[:idx], nil
   579→}
   580→
   581→// GetGitCommonDir returns the path to the shared git directory.
   582→// In a regular checkout, this is .git/
   583→// In a worktree, this is the main repo's .git/ (not .git/worktrees/<name>/)
   584→// Uses git rev-parse --git-common-dir for reliable handling of worktrees.
   585→func GetGitCommonDir() (string, error) {
   586→	ctx := context.Background()
   587→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "rev-parse", "--git-common-dir")
   588→	cmd.Dir = "."
   589→	output, err := cmd.Output()
   590→	if err != nil {
   591→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get git common dir: %w", err)
   592→	}
   593→
   594→	commonDir := strings.TrimSpace(string(output))
   595→
   596→	// git rev-parse --git-common-dir returns relative paths from the working directory,
   597→	// so we need to make it absolute if it isn't already
   598→	if !filepath.IsAbs(commonDir) {
   599→		commonDir = filepath.Join(".", commonDir)
   600→	}
   601→
   602→	return filepath.Clean(commonDir), nil
   603→}
   604→
   605→// GetWorktreePath returns the absolute path to the current worktree root.
   606→// This is the working directory path, not the git directory.
   607→func GetWorktreePath() (string, error) {
   608→	ctx := context.Background()
   609→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "rev-parse", "--show-toplevel")
   610→	output, err := cmd.Output()
   611→	if err != nil {
   612→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get worktree path: %w", err)
   613→	}
   614→	return strings.TrimSpace(string(output)), nil
   615→}
   616→
   617→// EnsureEntireGitignore ensures all required entries are in .entire/.gitignore
   618→// Works correctly from any subdirectory within the repository.
   619→func EnsureEntireGitignore() error {
   620→	// Get absolute path for the gitignore file
   621→	gitignoreAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(entireGitignore)
   622→	if err != nil {
   623→		gitignoreAbs = entireGitignore // Fallback to relative
   624→	}
   625→
   626→	// Read existing content
   627→	var content string
   628→	if data, err := os.ReadFile(gitignoreAbs); err == nil { //nolint:gosec // path is from AbsPath or constant
   629→		content = string(data)
   630→	}
   631→
   632→	// All entries that should be in .entire/.gitignore
   633→	requiredEntries := []string{
   634→		"tmp/",
   635→		"settings.local.json",
   636→		"metadata/",
   637→		"logs/",
   638→	}
   639→
   640→	// Track what needs to be added
   641→	var toAdd []string
   642→	for _, entry := range requiredEntries {
   643→		if !strings.Contains(content, entry) {
   644→			toAdd = append(toAdd, entry)
   645→		}
   646→	}
   647→
   648→	// Nothing to add
   649→	if len(toAdd) == 0 {
   650→		return nil
   651→	}
   652→
   653→	// Ensure .entire directory exists
   654→	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(gitignoreAbs), 0o750); err != nil {
   655→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create .entire directory: %w", err)
   656→	}
   657→
   658→	// Append missing entries to gitignore
   659→	var sb strings.Builder
   660→	for _, entry := range toAdd {
   661→		sb.WriteString(entry + "\n")
   662→	}
   663→	content += sb.String()
   664→
   665→	if err := os.WriteFile(gitignoreAbs, []byte(content), 0o644); err != nil { //nolint:gosec // path is from AbsPath or constant
   666→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write gitignore: %w", err)
   667→	}
   668→	return nil
   669→}
   670→
   671→// checkCanRewind checks if working directory is clean enough for rewind.
   672→// Returns (canRewind, reason, error). Shared by shadow and linear-shadow strategies.
   673→func checkCanRewind() (bool, string, error) {
   674→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   675→	if err != nil {
   676→		return false, "", fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
   677→	}
   678→
   679→	worktree, err := repo.Worktree()
   680→	if err != nil {
   681→		return false, "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get worktree: %w", err)
   682→	}
   683→
   684→	status, err := worktree.Status()
   685→	if err != nil {
   686→		return false, "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get status: %w", err)
   687→	}
   688→
   689→	if status.IsClean() {
   690→		return true, "", nil
   691→	}
   692→
   693→	var modified, added, deleted []string
   694→	for file, st := range status {
   695→		// Skip .entire directory
   696→		if paths.IsInfrastructurePath(file) {
   697→			continue
   698→		}
   699→
   700→		// Skip untracked files
   701→		if st.Worktree == git.Untracked {
   702→			continue
   703→		}
   704→
   705→		switch {
   706→		case st.Staging == git.Added || st.Worktree == git.Added:
   707→			added = append(added, file)
   708→		case st.Staging == git.Deleted || st.Worktree == git.Deleted:
   709→			deleted = append(deleted, file)
   710→		case st.Staging == git.Modified || st.Worktree == git.Modified:
   711→			modified = append(modified, file)
   712→		}
   713→	}
   714→
   715→	if len(modified) == 0 && len(added) == 0 && len(deleted) == 0 {
   716→		return true, "", nil
   717→	}
   718→
   719→	var msg strings.Builder
   720→	msg.WriteString("You have uncommitted changes:\n")
   721→	for _, f := range modified {
   722→		msg.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("  modified:   %s\n", f))
   723→	}
   724→	for _, f := range added {
   725→		msg.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("  added:      %s\n", f))
   726→	}
   727→	for _, f := range deleted {
   728→		msg.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("  deleted:    %s\n", f))
   729→	}
   730→	msg.WriteString("\nPlease commit or stash your changes before rewinding.")
   731→
   732→	return false, msg.String(), nil
   733→}
   734→
   735→// checkCanRewindWithWarning checks working directory and returns a warning with diff stats.
   736→// Unlike checkCanRewind, this always returns canRewind=true but includes a warning message
   737→// with +/- line stats for uncommitted changes. Used by manual-commit strategy.
   738→func checkCanRewindWithWarning() (bool, string, error) {
   739→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   740→	if err != nil {
   741→		// Can't open repo - still allow rewind but without stats
   742→		return true, "", nil //nolint:nilerr // Rewind allowed even if repo can't be opened
   743→	}
   744→
   745→	worktree, err := repo.Worktree()
   746→	if err != nil {
   747→		return true, "", nil //nolint:nilerr // Rewind allowed even if worktree can't be accessed
   748→	}
   749→
   750→	status, err := worktree.Status()
   751→	if err != nil {
   752→		return true, "", nil //nolint:nilerr // Rewind allowed even if status can't be retrieved
   753→	}
   754→
   755→	if status.IsClean() {
   756→		return true, "", nil
   757→	}
   758→
   759→	// Get HEAD commit tree for comparison - if we can't get it, just return without stats
   760→	head, err := repo.Head()
   761→	if err != nil {
   762→		return true, "", nil //nolint:nilerr // Rewind allowed even without HEAD (e.g., empty repo)
   763→	}
   764→
   765→	headCommit, err := repo.CommitObject(head.Hash())
   766→	if err != nil {
   767→		return true, "", nil //nolint:nilerr // Rewind allowed even if commit lookup fails
   768→	}
   769→
   770→	headTree, err := headCommit.Tree()
   771→	if err != nil {
   772→		return true, "", nil //nolint:nilerr // Rewind allowed even if tree lookup fails
   773→	}
   774→
   775→	type fileChange struct {
   776→		status   string // "modified", "added", "deleted"
   777→		added    int
   778→		removed  int
   779→		filename string
   780→	}
   781→
   782→	var changes []fileChange
   783→	// Use repo root, not cwd - git status returns paths relative to repo root
   784→	repoRoot, err := GetWorktreePath()
   785→	if err != nil {
   786→		return true, "", nil //nolint:nilerr // Rewind allowed even if repo root lookup fails
   787→	}
   788→
   789→	for file, st := range status {
   790→		// Skip .entire directory
   791→		if paths.IsInfrastructurePath(file) {
   792→			continue
   793→		}
   794→
   795→		// Skip untracked files
   796→		if st.Worktree == git.Untracked {
   797→			continue
   798→		}
   799→
   800→		var change fileChange
   801→		change.filename = file
   802→
   803→		switch {
   804→		case st.Staging == git.Added || st.Worktree == git.Added:
   805→			change.status = "added"
   806→			// New file - count all lines as added
   807→			absPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, file)
   808→			if content, err := os.ReadFile(absPath); err == nil { //nolint:gosec // absPath is repo root + relative path from git status
   809→				change.added = countLines(content)
   810→			}
   811→		case st.Staging == git.Deleted || st.Worktree == git.Deleted:
   812→			change.status = "deleted"
   813→			// Deleted file - count lines from HEAD as removed
   814→			if entry, err := headTree.File(file); err == nil {
   815→				if content, err := entry.Contents(); err == nil {
   816→					change.removed = countLines([]byte(content))
   817→				}
   818→			}
   819→		case st.Staging == git.Modified || st.Worktree == git.Modified:
   820→			change.status = "modified"
   821→			// Modified file - compute diff stats
   822→			var headContent, workContent []byte
   823→			if entry, err := headTree.File(file); err == nil {
   824→				if content, err := entry.Contents(); err == nil {
   825→					headContent = []byte(content)
   826→				}
   827→			}
   828→			absPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, file)
   829→			if content, err := os.ReadFile(absPath); err == nil { //nolint:gosec // absPath is repo root + relative path from git status
   830→				workContent = content
   831→			}
   832→			if headContent != nil && workContent != nil {
   833→				change.added, change.removed = computeDiffStats(headContent, workContent)
   834→			}
   835→		default:
   836→			continue
   837→		}
   838→
   839→		changes = append(changes, change)
   840→	}
   841→
   842→	if len(changes) == 0 {
   843→		return true, "", nil
   844→	}
   845→
   846→	// Sort changes by filename for consistent output
   847→	sort.Slice(changes, func(i, j int) bool {
   848→		return changes[i].filename < changes[j].filename
   849→	})
   850→
   851→	var msg strings.Builder
   852→	msg.WriteString("The following uncommitted changes will be reverted:\n")
   853→
   854→	totalAdded, totalRemoved := 0, 0
   855→	for _, c := range changes {
   856→		totalAdded += c.added
   857→		totalRemoved += c.removed
   858→
   859→		var stats string
   860→		switch {
   861→		case c.added > 0 && c.removed > 0:
   862→			stats = fmt.Sprintf("+%d/-%d", c.added, c.removed)
   863→		case c.added > 0:
   864→			stats = fmt.Sprintf("+%d", c.added)
   865→		case c.removed > 0:
   866→			stats = fmt.Sprintf("-%d", c.removed)
   867→		}
   868→
   869→		msg.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("  %-10s %s", c.status+":", c.filename))
   870→		if stats != "" {
   871→			msg.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf(" (%s)", stats))
   872→		}
   873→		msg.WriteString("\n")
   874→	}
   875→
   876→	if totalAdded > 0 || totalRemoved > 0 {
   877→		msg.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("\nTotal: +%d/-%d lines\n", totalAdded, totalRemoved))
   878→	}
   879→
   880→	return true, msg.String(), nil
   881→}
   882→
   883→// countLines counts the number of lines in content.
   884→func countLines(content []byte) int {
   885→	if len(content) == 0 {
   886→		return 0
   887→	}
   888→	count := 1
   889→	for _, b := range content {
   890→		if b == '\n' {
   891→			count++
   892→		}
   893→	}
   894→	// Don't count trailing newline as extra line
   895→	if len(content) > 0 && content[len(content)-1] == '\n' {
   896→		count--
   897→	}
   898→	return count
   899→}
   900→
   901→// computeDiffStats computes added and removed line counts between old and new content.
   902→// Uses a simple line-based diff algorithm.
   903→func computeDiffStats(oldContent, newContent []byte) (added, removed int) {
   904→	oldLines := splitLines(oldContent)
   905→	newLines := splitLines(newContent)
   906→
   907→	// Build a set of old lines with counts
   908→	oldSet := make(map[string]int)
   909→	for _, line := range oldLines {
   910→		oldSet[line]++
   911→	}
   912→
   913→	// Check which new lines are truly new
   914→	for _, line := range newLines {
   915→		if oldSet[line] > 0 {
   916→			oldSet[line]--
   917→		} else {
   918→			added++
   919→		}
   920→	}
   921→
   922→	// Remaining old lines are removed
   923→	for _, count := range oldSet {
   924→		removed += count
   925→	}
   926→
   927→	return added, removed
   928→}
   929→
   930→// splitLines splits content into lines, preserving empty lines.
   931→func splitLines(content []byte) []string {
   932→	if len(content) == 0 {
   933→		return nil
   934→	}
   935→	s := string(content)
   936→	// Remove trailing newline to avoid empty last element
   937→	s = strings.TrimSuffix(s, "\n")
   938→	return strings.Split(s, "\n")
   939→}
   940→
   941→// fileExists checks if a file exists at the given path.
   942→func fileExists(path string) bool {
   943→	_, err := os.Stat(path)
   944→	return err == nil
   945→}
   946→
   947→// StageFilesContext describes what type of staging operation this is (for messages).
   948→type StageFilesContext string
   949→
   950→const (
   951→	// StageForSession is used when staging files for a session checkpoint.
   952→	StageForSession StageFilesContext = "session"
   953→	// StageForTask is used when staging files for a task checkpoint.
   954→	StageForTask StageFilesContext = "task"
   955→)
   956→
   957→// StageFiles stages modified, new, and deleted files to the git worktree.
   958→//
   959→// This function handles three categories of file changes:
   960→//  1. Modified files: existing files that have been changed
   961→//  2. New files: files that were created during the session
   962→//  3. Deleted files: files that were removed during the session
   963→//
   964→// Error Handling Strategy:
   965→//   - Individual file staging errors are logged to stderr but don't fail the operation
   966→//   - This ensures that partial staging succeeds even if some files have issues
   967→//   - If a modified file no longer exists, it's treated as a deletion
   968→//
   969→// The stageCtx parameter is used for user-facing messages to indicate whether
   970→// this is staging for a session checkpoint or a task checkpoint.
   971→func StageFiles(worktree *git.Worktree, modified, newFiles, deleted []string, stageCtx StageFilesContext) {
   972→	// Get repo root for resolving file paths
   973→	// This is critical because fileExists() uses os.Stat() which resolves relative to CWD,
   974→	// but worktree.Add/Remove resolve relative to repo root. If CWD != repo root,
   975→	// fileExists() could return false for existing files, causing worktree.Remove()
   976→	// to incorrectly delete them.
   977→	repoRoot := worktree.Filesystem.Root()
   978→
   979→	// Stage modified files
   980→	for _, file := range modified {
   981→		// Resolve path relative to repo root for existence check
   982→		absPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, file)
   983→		if fileExists(absPath) {
   984→			if _, err := worktree.Add(file); err != nil {
   985→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Failed to stage %s: %v\n", file, err)
   986→			} else {
   987→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Staged: %s\n", file)
   988→			}
   989→		} else {
   990→			// File was deleted - stage the deletion
   991→			if _, err := worktree.Remove(file); err != nil {
   992→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  File not found or deleted: %s\n", file)
   993→			}
   994→		}
   995→	}
   996→
   997→	// Stage new files
   998→	if len(newFiles) > 0 {
   999→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Staging %d new files created during %s:\n", len(newFiles), stageCtx)
  1000→		for _, file := range newFiles {
  1001→			if _, err := worktree.Add(file); err != nil {
  1002→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Failed to stage %s: %v\n", file, err)
  1003→			} else {
  1004→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Staged new file: %s\n", file)
  1005→			}
  1006→		}
  1007→	}
  1008→
  1009→	// Stage deleted files
  1010→	for _, file := range deleted {
  1011→		if _, err := worktree.Remove(file); err != nil {
  1012→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Failed to stage deleted file %s: %v\n", file, err)
  1013→		} else {
  1014→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Staged deleted file: %s\n", file)
  1015→		}
  1016→	}
  1017→}
  1018→
  1019→// getTaskCheckpointFromTree retrieves a task checkpoint from a commit tree.
  1020→// Shared implementation for shadow and linear-shadow strategies.
  1021→func getTaskCheckpointFromTree(point RewindPoint) (*TaskCheckpoint, error) {
  1022→	if !point.IsTaskCheckpoint {
  1023→		return nil, ErrNotTaskCheckpoint
  1024→	}
  1025→
  1026→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
  1027→	if err != nil {
  1028→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to open repository: %w", err)
  1029→	}
  1030→
  1031→	commitHash := plumbing.NewHash(point.ID)
  1032→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(commitHash)
  1033→	if err != nil {
  1034→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit: %w", err)
  1035→	}
  1036→
  1037→	tree, err := commit.Tree()
  1038→	if err != nil {
  1039→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get tree: %w", err)
  1040→	}
  1041→
  1042→	// Read checkpoint.json from the tree
  1043→	checkpointPath := point.MetadataDir + "/checkpoint.json"
  1044→	file, err := tree.File(checkpointPath)
  1045→	if err != nil {
  1046→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to find checkpoint at %s: %w", checkpointPath, err)
  1047→	}
  1048→
  1049→	content, err := file.Contents()
  1050→	if err != nil {
  1051→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read checkpoint: %w", err)
  1052→	}
  1053→
  1054→	var checkpoint TaskCheckpoint
  1055→	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(content), &checkpoint); err != nil {
  1056→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse checkpoint: %w", err)
  1057→	}
  1058→
  1059→	return &checkpoint, nil
  1060→}

  1062→// getTaskTranscriptFromTree retrieves a task transcript from a commit tree.
  1063→// Shared implementation for shadow and linear-shadow strategies.
  1064→func getTaskTranscriptFromTree(point RewindPoint) ([]byte, error) {
  1065→	if !point.IsTaskCheckpoint {
  1066→		return nil, ErrNotTaskCheckpoint
  1067→	}
  1068→
  1069→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
  1070→	if err != nil {
  1071→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to open repository: %w", err)
  1072→	}
  1073→
  1074→	commitHash := plumbing.NewHash(point.ID)
  1075→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(commitHash)
  1076→	if err != nil {
  1077→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit: %w", err)
  1078→	}
  1079→
  1080→	tree, err := commit.Tree()
  1081→	if err != nil {
  1082→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get tree: %w", err)
  1083→	}
  1084→
  1085→	// MetadataDir format: .entire/metadata/<session>/tasks/<toolUseID>
  1086→	// Session transcript is at: .entire/metadata/<session>/<TranscriptFileName>
  1087→	sessionDir := filepath.Dir(filepath.Dir(point.MetadataDir))
  1088→
  1089→	// Try current format first, then legacy
  1090→	transcriptPath := sessionDir + "/" + paths.TranscriptFileName
  1091→	file, err := tree.File(transcriptPath)
  1092→	if err != nil {
  1093→		transcriptPath = sessionDir + "/" + paths.TranscriptFileNameLegacy
  1094→		file, err = tree.File(transcriptPath)
  1095→		if err != nil {
  1096→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to find transcript: %w", err)
  1097→		}
  1098→	}
  1099→
  1100→	content, err := file.Contents()
  1101→	if err != nil {
  1102→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
  1103→	}
  1104→
  1105→	return []byte(content), nil
  1106→}
  1107→
  1108→// HardResetWithProtection performs a git reset --hard to the specified commit.
  1109→// Uses the git CLI instead of go-git because go-git's HardReset incorrectly
  1110→// deletes untracked directories (like .entire/) even when they're in .gitignore.
  1111→// Returns the short commit ID (7 chars) on success for display purposes.
  1112→func HardResetWithProtection(commitHash plumbing.Hash) (shortID string, err error) {
  1113→	ctx := context.Background()
  1114→	hashStr := commitHash.String()
  1115→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "reset", "--hard", hashStr) //nolint:gosec // hashStr is a plumbing.Hash, not user input
  1116→	if output, err := cmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
  1117→		return "", fmt.Errorf("reset failed: %s: %w", strings.TrimSpace(string(output)), err)
  1118→	}
  1119→
  1120→	// Return short commit ID for display
  1121→	shortID = hashStr
  1122→	if len(shortID) > 7 {
  1123→		shortID = shortID[:7]
  1124→	}
  1125→	return shortID, nil
  1126→}
  1127→
  1128→// collectUntrackedFiles collects all untracked files in the working directory.
  1129→// This is used to capture the initial state when starting a session,
  1130→// ensuring untracked files present at session start are preserved during rewind.
  1131→// Excludes .git/, .entire/, and .claude/ directories (which are system/config directories).
  1132→// Works correctly from any subdirectory within the repository.
  1133→func collectUntrackedFiles() ([]string, error) {
  1134→	// Get repository root to walk from there
  1135→	repoRoot, err := GetWorktreePath()
  1136→	if err != nil {
  1137→		repoRoot = "." // Fallback to current directory
  1138→	}
  1139→
  1140→	var untrackedFiles []string
  1141→	err = filepath.Walk(repoRoot, func(path string, info os.FileInfo, walkErr error) error {
  1142→		if walkErr != nil {
  1143→			return nil //nolint:nilerr // Skip filesystem errors during walk
  1144→		}
  1145→
  1146→		// Get path relative to repo root
  1147→		relPath, err := filepath.Rel(repoRoot, path)
  1148→		if err != nil {
  1149→			return nil //nolint:nilerr // Skip paths we can't make relative
  1150→		}
  1151→
  1152→		// Skip directories
  1153→		if info.IsDir() {
  1154→			// Skip .git, .claude, and .entire directories
  1155→			if relPath == gitDir || relPath == claudeDir || relPath == entireDir ||
  1156→				strings.HasPrefix(relPath, gitDir+"/") || strings.HasPrefix(relPath, claudeDir+"/") || strings.HasPrefix(relPath, entireDir+"/") {
  1157→				return filepath.SkipDir
  1158→			}
  1159→			return nil
  1160→		}
  1161→
  1162→		// Skip files in special directories (shouldn't reach here due to SkipDir, but safety check)
  1163→		if strings.HasPrefix(relPath, gitDir+"/") || strings.HasPrefix(relPath, claudeDir+"/") || strings.HasPrefix(relPath, entireDir+"/") {
  1164→			return nil
  1165→		}
  1166→
  1167→		untrackedFiles = append(untrackedFiles, relPath)
  1168→		return nil
  1169→	})
  1170→	if err != nil {
  1171→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to walk directory: %w", err)
  1172→	}
  1173→
  1174→	return untrackedFiles, nil
  1175→}
  1176→
  1177→// ExtractSessionIDFromCommit extracts the session ID from a commit's trailers.
  1178→// It checks the Entire-Session trailer first, then falls back to extracting from
  1179→// the metadata directory path in the Entire-Metadata trailer.
  1180→// Returns empty string if no session ID is found.
  1181→func ExtractSessionIDFromCommit(commit *object.Commit) string {
  1182→	// Try Entire-Session trailer first
  1183→	if sessionID, found := trailers.ParseSession(commit.Message); found {
  1184→		return sessionID
  1185→	}
  1186→
  1187→	// Try extracting from metadata directory (last path component)
  1188→	if metadataDir, found := trailers.ParseMetadata(commit.Message); found {
  1189→		return filepath.Base(metadataDir)
  1190→	}
  1191→
  1192→	return ""
  1193→}
  1194→
  1195→// NOTE: The following git tree helper functions have been moved to checkpoint/ package:
  1196→// - FlattenTree -> checkpoint.FlattenTree
  1197→// - CreateBlobFromContent -> checkpoint.CreateBlobFromContent
  1198→// - BuildTreeFromEntries -> checkpoint.BuildTreeFromEntries
  1199→// - sortTreeEntries (internal to checkpoint package)
  1200→// - treeNode, insertIntoTree, buildTreeObject (internal to checkpoint package)
  1201→//
  1202→// See push_common.go and session_test.go for usage examples.
  1203→
  1204→// createCommit creates a commit object
  1205→func createCommit(repo *git.Repository, treeHash, parentHash plumbing.Hash, message, authorName, authorEmail string) (plumbing.Hash, error) {
  1206→	now := time.Now()
  1207→	sig := object.Signature{
  1208→		Name:  authorName,
  1209→		Email: authorEmail,
  1210→		When:  now,
  1211→	}
  1212→
  1213→	commit := &object.Commit{
  1214→		TreeHash:  treeHash,
  1215→		Author:    sig,
  1216→		Committer: sig,
  1217→		Message:   message,
  1218→	}
  1219→
  1220→	// Add parent if not a new branch
  1221→	if parentHash != plumbing.ZeroHash {
  1222→		commit.ParentHashes = []plumbing.Hash{parentHash}
  1223→	}
  1224→
  1225→	obj := repo.Storer.NewEncodedObject()
  1226→	if err := commit.Encode(obj); err != nil {
  1227→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to encode commit: %w", err)
  1228→	}
  1229→
  1230→	hash, err := repo.Storer.SetEncodedObject(obj)
  1231→	if err != nil {
  1232→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to store commit: %w", err)
  1233→	}
  1234→
  1235→	return hash, nil
  1236→}
  1237→
  1238→// getSessionDescriptionFromTree reads the first line of prompt.txt or context.md from a git tree.
  1239→// This is the tree-based equivalent of getSessionDescription (which reads from filesystem).
  1240→//
  1241→// If metadataDir is provided, looks for files at metadataDir/prompt.txt or metadataDir/context.md.
  1242→// If metadataDir is empty, first tries the root of the tree (for when the tree is already
  1243→// the session directory, e.g., auto-commit strategy's sharded metadata), then falls back to
  1244→// searching for .entire/metadata/*/prompt.txt or context.md (for full worktree trees).
  1245→func getSessionDescriptionFromTree(tree *object.Tree, metadataDir string) string {
  1246→	// Helper to read first line from a file in tree
  1247→	readFirstLine := func(path string) string {
  1248→		file, err := tree.File(path)
  1249→		if err != nil {
  1250→			return ""
  1251→		}
  1252→		content, err := file.Contents()
  1253→		if err != nil {
  1254→			return ""
  1255→		}
  1256→		lines := strings.SplitN(content, "\n", 2)
  1257→		if len(lines) > 0 && lines[0] != "" {
  1258→			desc := strings.TrimSpace(lines[0])
  1259→			// Remove markdown header prefix if present
  1260→			return strings.TrimPrefix(desc, "# ")
  1261→		}
  1262→		return ""
  1263→	}
  1264→
  1265→	// If metadataDir is provided, look there directly
  1266→	if metadataDir != "" {
  1267→		if desc := readFirstLine(metadataDir + "/" + paths.PromptFileName); desc != "" {
  1268→			return desc
  1269→		}
  1270→		if desc := readFirstLine(metadataDir + "/" + paths.ContextFileName); desc != "" {
  1271→			return desc
  1272→		}
  1273→		return NoDescription
  1274→	}
  1275→
  1276→	// No metadataDir provided - first try looking at the root of the tree
  1277→	// (used when the tree is already the session directory)
  1278→	if desc := readFirstLine(paths.PromptFileName); desc != "" {
  1279→		return desc
  1280→	}
  1281→	if desc := readFirstLine(paths.ContextFileName); desc != "" {
  1282→		return desc
  1283→	}
  1284→
  1285→	// Fall back to searching for .entire/metadata/*/prompt.txt or context.md
  1286→	// (used when the tree is the full worktree)
  1287→	var desc string
  1288→	//nolint:errcheck // We ignore errors here as we're just searching for a description
  1289→	_ = tree.Files().ForEach(func(f *object.File) error {
  1290→		if desc != "" {
  1291→			return nil // Already found description
  1292→		}
  1293→		name := f.Name
  1294→		if strings.Contains(name, ".entire/metadata/") {
  1295→			if strings.HasSuffix(name, "/"+paths.PromptFileName) || strings.HasSuffix(name, "/"+paths.ContextFileName) {
  1296→				content, err := f.Contents()
  1297→				if err != nil {
  1298→					return nil //nolint:nilerr // Skip files we can't read, continue searching
  1299→				}
  1300→				lines := strings.SplitN(content, "\n", 2)
  1301→				if len(lines) > 0 && lines[0] != "" {
  1302→					desc = strings.TrimSpace(lines[0])
  1303→					desc = strings.TrimPrefix(desc, "# ")
  1304→				}
  1305→			}
  1306→		}
  1307→		return nil
  1308→	})
  1309→
  1310→	if desc != "" {
  1311→		return desc
  1312→	}
  1313→	return NoDescription
  1314→}
  1315→
  1316→// GetGitAuthorFromRepo retrieves the git user.name and user.email from the repository config.
  1317→// It checks local config first, then falls back to global config.
  1318→// Returns ("Unknown", "unknown@local") if no user is configured - this allows
  1319→// operations to proceed even without git user config, which is especially useful
  1320→// for internal metadata commits on branches like entire/sessions.
  1321→func GetGitAuthorFromRepo(repo *git.Repository) (name, email string) {
  1322→	// Get repository config (includes local settings)
  1323→	cfg, err := repo.Config()
  1324→	if err == nil {
  1325→		name = cfg.User.Name
  1326→		email = cfg.User.Email
  1327→	}
  1328→
  1329→	// If not found in local config, try global config
  1330→	if name == "" || email == "" {
  1331→		globalCfg, err := config.LoadConfig(config.GlobalScope)
  1332→		if err == nil {
  1333→			if name == "" {
  1334→				name = globalCfg.User.Name
  1335→			}
  1336→			if email == "" {
  1337→				email = globalCfg.User.Email
  1338→			}
  1339→		}
  1340→	}
  1341→
  1342→	// Provide sensible defaults if git user is not configured
  1343→	if name == "" {
  1344→		name = "Unknown"
  1345→	}
  1346→	if email == "" {
  1347→		email = "unknown@local"
  1348→	}
  1349→
  1350→	return name, email
  1351→}
  1352→
  1353→// GetCurrentBranchName returns the short name of the current branch if HEAD points to a branch.
  1354→// Returns an empty string if in detached HEAD state or if there's an error reading HEAD.
  1355→// This is used to capture branch metadata for checkpoints.
  1356→func GetCurrentBranchName(repo *git.Repository) string {
  1357→	head, err := repo.Head()
  1358→	if err != nil || !head.Name().IsBranch() {
  1359→		return ""
  1360→	}
  1361→	return head.Name().Short()
  1362→}
  1363→
  1364→// getMainBranchHash returns the hash of the main branch (main or master).
  1365→// Returns ZeroHash if no main branch is found.
  1366→func GetMainBranchHash(repo *git.Repository) plumbing.Hash {
  1367→	// Try common main branch names
  1368→	for _, branchName := range []string{branchMain, branchMaster} {
  1369→		// Try local branch first
  1370→		ref, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(branchName), true)
  1371→		if err == nil {
  1372→			return ref.Hash()
  1373→		}
  1374→		// Try remote tracking branch
  1375→		ref, err = repo.Reference(plumbing.NewRemoteReferenceName("origin", branchName), true)
  1376→		if err == nil {
  1377→			return ref.Hash()
  1378→		}
  1379→	}
  1380→	return plumbing.ZeroHash
  1381→}
  1382→
  1383→// GetDefaultBranchName returns the name of the default branch.
  1384→// First checks origin/HEAD, then falls back to checking if main/master exists.
  1385→// Returns empty string if unable to determine.
  1386→// NOTE: Duplicated from cli/git_operations.go - see ENT-129 for consolidation.
  1387→func GetDefaultBranchName(repo *git.Repository) string {
  1388→	// Try to get the symbolic reference for origin/HEAD
  1389→	// Use resolved=false to get the symbolic ref itself, then extract its target
  1390→	ref, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewRemoteReferenceName("origin", "HEAD"), false)
  1391→	if err == nil && ref != nil && ref.Type() == plumbing.SymbolicReference {
  1392→		target := ref.Target().String()
  1393→		if branchName, found := strings.CutPrefix(target, "refs/remotes/origin/"); found {
  1394→			return branchName
  1395→		}
  1396→	}
  1397→
  1398→	// Fallback: check if origin/main or origin/master exists
  1399→	if _, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewRemoteReferenceName("origin", branchMain), true); err == nil {
  1400→		return branchMain
  1401→	}
  1402→	if _, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewRemoteReferenceName("origin", branchMaster), true); err == nil {
  1403→		return branchMaster
  1404→	}
  1405→
  1406→	// Final fallback: check local branches
  1407→	if _, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(branchMain), true); err == nil {
  1408→		return branchMain
  1409→	}
  1410→	if _, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(branchMaster), true); err == nil {
  1411→		return branchMaster
  1412→	}
  1413→
  1414→	return ""
  1415→}
  1416→
  1417→// IsOnDefaultBranch checks if the repository HEAD is on the default branch.
  1418→// Returns (isOnDefault, currentBranchName).
  1419→// NOTE: Duplicated from cli/git_operations.go - see ENT-129 for consolidation.
  1420→func IsOnDefaultBranch(repo *git.Repository) (bool, string) {
  1421→	currentBranch := GetCurrentBranchName(repo)
  1422→	if currentBranch == "" {
  1423→		return false, ""
  1424→	}
  1425→
  1426→	defaultBranch := GetDefaultBranchName(repo)
  1427→	if defaultBranch == "" {
  1428→		// Can't determine default, check common names
  1429→		if currentBranch == branchMain || currentBranch == branchMaster {
  1430→			return true, currentBranch
  1431→		}
  1432→		return false, currentBranch
  1433→	}
  1434→
  1435→	return currentBranch == defaultBranch, currentBranch
  1436→}

    39→// CheckpointPath returns the sharded storage path for a checkpoint ID.
    40→// Uses first 2 characters as shard (256 buckets), remaining as folder name.
    41→// Example: "a3b2c4d5e6f7" -> "a3/b2c4d5e6f7"

    47→
    48→// repoRootCache caches the repository root to avoid repeated git commands.
    49→// The cache is keyed by the current working directory to handle directory changes.
    50→var (
    51→	repoRootMu       sync.RWMutex
    52→	repoRootCache    string
    53→	repoRootCacheDir string
    54→)
    55→
    56→// RepoRoot returns the git repository root directory.
    57→// Uses 'git rev-parse --show-toplevel' which works from any subdirectory.
    58→// The result is cached per working directory.
    59→// Returns an error if not inside a git repository.
    60→func RepoRoot() (string, error) {
    61→	// Get current working directory to check cache validity
    62→	cwd, err := os.Getwd()
    63→	if err != nil {
    64→		cwd = ""
    65→	}
    66→
    67→	// Check cache with read lock first
    68→	repoRootMu.RLock()
    69→	if repoRootCache != "" && repoRootCacheDir == cwd {
    70→		cached := repoRootCache
    71→		repoRootMu.RUnlock()
    72→		return cached, nil
    73→	}
    74→	repoRootMu.RUnlock()
    75→
    76→	// Cache miss - get repo root and update cache with write lock
    77→	ctx := context.Background()
    78→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "rev-parse", "--show-toplevel")
    79→	output, err := cmd.Output()
    80→	if err != nil {
    81→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get git repository root: %w", err)
    82→	}
    83→
    84→	root := strings.TrimSpace(string(output))
    85→
    86→	repoRootMu.Lock()
    87→	repoRootCache = root
    88→	repoRootCacheDir = cwd
    89→	repoRootMu.Unlock()
    90→
    91→	return root, nil
    92→}
    93→
    94→// ClearRepoRootCache clears the cached repository root.
    95→// This is primarily useful for testing when changing directories.
    96→func ClearRepoRootCache() {
    97→	repoRootMu.Lock()
    98→	repoRootCache = ""
    99→	repoRootCacheDir = ""
   100→	repoRootMu.Unlock()
   101→}
   102→
   103→// RepoRootOr returns the git repository root directory, or the current directory
   104→// if not inside a git repository. This is useful for functions that need a fallback.
   105→func RepoRootOr(fallback string) string {
   106→	root, err := RepoRoot()
   107→	if err != nil {
   108→		return fallback
   109→	}
   110→	return root
   111→}
   112→
   113→// AbsPath returns the absolute path for a relative path within the repository.
   114→// If the path is already absolute, it is returned as-is.
   115→// Uses RepoRoot() to resolve paths relative to the repository root.
   116→func AbsPath(relPath string) (string, error) {
   117→	if filepath.IsAbs(relPath) {
   118→		return relPath, nil
   119→	}
   120→
   121→	root, err := RepoRoot()
   122→	if err != nil {
   123→		return "", err
   124→	}
   125→
   126→	return filepath.Join(root, relPath), nil
   127→}
   128→
   129→// IsInfrastructurePath returns true if the path is part of CLI infrastructure
   130→// (i.e., inside the .entire directory)
   131→func IsInfrastructurePath(path string) bool {
   132→	return strings.HasPrefix(path, EntireDir+"/") || path == EntireDir
   133→}

   135→// ToRelativePath converts an absolute path to relative.
   136→// Returns empty string if the path is outside the working directory.
   137→func ToRelativePath(absPath, cwd string) string {
   138→	if !filepath.IsAbs(absPath) {
   139→		return absPath
   140→	}
   141→	relPath, err := filepath.Rel(cwd, absPath)
   142→	if err != nil || strings.HasPrefix(relPath, "..") {
   143→		return ""
   144→	}
   145→	return relPath
   146→}

   149→var nonAlphanumericRegex = regexp.MustCompile(`[^a-zA-Z0-9]`)
   150→
   151→// SanitizePathForClaude converts a path to Claude's project directory format.
   152→// Claude replaces any non-alphanumeric character with a dash.
   153→func SanitizePathForClaude(path string) string {
   154→	return nonAlphanumericRegex.ReplaceAllString(path, "-")
   155→}
   156→
   157→// GetClaudeProjectDir returns the directory where Claude stores session transcripts
   158→// for the given repository path.
   159→//
   160→// In test environments, set ENTIRE_TEST_CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR to override the default location.
   161→func GetClaudeProjectDir(repoPath string) (string, error) {
   162→	override := os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TEST_CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR")
   163→	if override != "" {
   164→		return override, nil
   165→	}
   166→
   167→	homeDir, err := os.UserHomeDir()
   168→	if err != nil {
   169→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get home directory: %w", err)
   170→	}
   171→
   172→	projectDir := SanitizePathForClaude(repoPath)
   173→	return filepath.Join(homeDir, ".claude", "projects", projectDir), nil
   174→}
   175→
   176→// SessionMetadataDirFromSessionID returns the path to a session's metadata directory
   177→// for the given Entire session ID. The sessionID must be the full, already date-prefixed
   178→// Entire session identifier as stored on disk, not an agent-specific or raw Claude ID.
   179→func SessionMetadataDirFromSessionID(sessionID string) string {
   180→	return EntireMetadataDir + "/" + sessionID
   181→}
   182→
   183→// ExtractSessionIDFromTranscriptPath attempts to extract a session ID from a transcript path.
   184→// Claude transcripts are stored at ~/.claude/projects/<project>/sessions/<id>.jsonl
   185→// If the path doesn't match expected format, returns empty string.
   186→func ExtractSessionIDFromTranscriptPath(transcriptPath string) string {
   187→	// Try to extract from typical path: ~/.claude/projects/<project>/sessions/<id>.jsonl
   188→	parts := strings.Split(filepath.ToSlash(transcriptPath), "/")
   189→	for i, part := range parts {
   190→		if part == "sessions" && i+1 < len(parts) {
   191→			// Return filename without extension
   192→			filename := parts[i+1]
   193→			if strings.HasSuffix(filename, ".jsonl") {
   194→				return strings.TrimSuffix(filename, ".jsonl")
   195→			}
   196→			return filename
   197→		}
   198→	}
   199→	return ""
   200→}
   201→

     1→//go:generate go run gen_state_diagram.go
