     3→package session
     4→
     5→import (
     6→	"fmt"
     7→	"strings"
     8→	"time"
     9→)
    10→
    11→// Phase represents the lifecycle stage of a session.
    12→type Phase string
    13→
    14→const (
    15→	PhaseActive          Phase = "active"
    16→	PhaseActiveCommitted Phase = "active_committed"
    17→	PhaseIdle            Phase = "idle"
    18→	PhaseEnded           Phase = "ended"
    19→)
    20→
    21→// allPhases is the canonical list of phases for enumeration (e.g., diagram generation).
    22→var allPhases = []Phase{PhaseIdle, PhaseActive, PhaseActiveCommitted, PhaseEnded}
    23→
    24→// PhaseFromString normalizes a phase string, treating empty or unknown values
    25→// as PhaseIdle for backward compatibility with pre-state-machine session files.
    26→func PhaseFromString(s string) Phase {
    27→	switch Phase(s) {
    28→	case PhaseActive:
    29→		return PhaseActive
    30→	case PhaseActiveCommitted:
    31→		return PhaseActiveCommitted
    32→	case PhaseIdle:
    33→		return PhaseIdle
    34→	case PhaseEnded:
    35→		return PhaseEnded
    36→	default:
    37→		return PhaseIdle
    38→	}
    39→}
    40→
    41→// IsActive reports whether the phase represents an active agent turn.
    42→func (p Phase) IsActive() bool {
    43→	return p == PhaseActive || p == PhaseActiveCommitted
    44→}
    45→
    46→// Event represents something that happened to a session.
    47→type Event int
    48→
    49→const (
    50→	EventTurnStart    Event = iota // Agent begins working on a prompt
    51→	EventTurnEnd                   // Agent finishes its turn
    52→	EventGitCommit                 // A git commit was made (PostCommit hook)
    53→	EventSessionStart              // Session process started (SessionStart hook)
    54→	EventSessionStop               // Session process ended (SessionStop hook)
    55→)
    56→
    57→// allEvents is the canonical list of events for enumeration.
    58→var allEvents = []Event{EventTurnStart, EventTurnEnd, EventGitCommit, EventSessionStart, EventSessionStop}
    59→
    60→// String returns a human-readable name for the event.
    61→func (e Event) String() string {
    62→	switch e {
    63→	case EventTurnStart:
    64→		return "TurnStart"
    65→	case EventTurnEnd:
    66→		return "TurnEnd"
    67→	case EventGitCommit:
    68→		return "GitCommit"
    69→	case EventSessionStart:
    70→		return "SessionStart"
    71→	case EventSessionStop:
    72→		return "SessionStop"
    73→	default:
    74→		return fmt.Sprintf("Event(%d)", int(e))
    75→	}
    76→}
    77→
    78→// Action represents a side effect that should be performed after a transition.
    79→// The caller is responsible for executing these -- the state machine only declares them.
    80→type Action int
    81→
    82→const (
    83→	ActionCondense               Action = iota // Condense session data to permanent storage
    84→	ActionCondenseIfFilesTouched               // Condense only if FilesTouched is non-empty
    85→	ActionDiscardIfNoFiles                     // Discard session if FilesTouched is empty
    86→	ActionMigrateShadowBranch                  // Migrate shadow branch to new HEAD
    87→	ActionWarnStaleSession                     // Warn user about stale session(s)
    88→	ActionClearEndedAt                         // Clear EndedAt timestamp (session re-entering)
    89→	ActionUpdateLastInteraction                // Update LastInteractionTime
    90→)
    91→
    92→// String returns a human-readable name for the action.
    93→func (a Action) String() string {
    94→	switch a {
    95→	case ActionCondense:
    96→		return "Condense"
    97→	case ActionCondenseIfFilesTouched:
    98→		return "CondenseIfFilesTouched"
    99→	case ActionDiscardIfNoFiles:
   100→		return "DiscardIfNoFiles"
   101→	case ActionMigrateShadowBranch:
   102→		return "MigrateShadowBranch"
   103→	case ActionWarnStaleSession:
   104→		return "WarnStaleSession"
   105→	case ActionClearEndedAt:
   106→		return "ClearEndedAt"
   107→	case ActionUpdateLastInteraction:
   108→		return "UpdateLastInteraction"
   109→	default:
   110→		return fmt.Sprintf("Action(%d)", int(a))
   111→	}
   112→}
   113→
   114→// TransitionContext provides read-only context for transitions that need
   115→// to inspect session state without mutating it.
   116→type TransitionContext struct {
   117→	HasFilesTouched    bool // len(FilesTouched) > 0
   118→	IsRebaseInProgress bool // .git/rebase-merge/ or .git/rebase-apply/ exists
   119→}
   120→
   121→// TransitionResult holds the outcome of a state machine transition.
   122→type TransitionResult struct {
   123→	NewPhase Phase
   124→	Actions  []Action
   125→}
   126→
   127→// Transition computes the next phase and required actions given the current
   128→// phase and an event. This is a pure function with no side effects.
   129→//
   130→// Empty or unknown phase values are normalized to PhaseIdle for backward
   131→// compatibility with session state files created before phase tracking.
   132→func Transition(current Phase, event Event, ctx TransitionContext) TransitionResult {
   133→	// Normalize empty/unknown phase to idle for backward compatibility.
   134→	current = PhaseFromString(string(current))
   135→
   136→	switch current {
   137→	case PhaseIdle:
   138→		return transitionFromIdle(event, ctx)
   139→	case PhaseActive:
   140→		return transitionFromActive(event, ctx)
   141→	case PhaseActiveCommitted:
   142→		return transitionFromActiveCommitted(event, ctx)
   143→	case PhaseEnded:
   144→		return transitionFromEnded(event, ctx)
   145→	default:
   146→		// PhaseFromString guarantees we only get known phases, but the
   147→		// exhaustive linter requires handling the default case.
   148→		return TransitionResult{NewPhase: PhaseIdle}
   149→	}
   150→}
   151→
   152→func transitionFromIdle(event Event, ctx TransitionContext) TransitionResult {
   153→	switch event {
   154→	case EventTurnStart:
   155→		return TransitionResult{
   156→			NewPhase: PhaseActive,
   157→			Actions:  []Action{ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
   158→		}
   159→	case EventTurnEnd:
   160→		// Turn end while idle is a no-op (no active turn to end).
   161→		return TransitionResult{NewPhase: PhaseIdle}
   162→	case EventGitCommit:
   163→		if ctx.IsRebaseInProgress {
   164→			return TransitionResult{NewPhase: PhaseIdle}
   165→		}
   166→		return TransitionResult{
   167→			NewPhase: PhaseIdle,
   168→			Actions:  []Action{ActionCondense, ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
   169→		}
   170→	case EventSessionStart:
   171→		// Already condensable, no-op.
   172→		return TransitionResult{NewPhase: PhaseIdle}
   173→	case EventSessionStop:
   174→		return TransitionResult{
   175→			NewPhase: PhaseEnded,
   176→			Actions:  []Action{ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
   177→		}
   178→	default:
   179→		return TransitionResult{NewPhase: PhaseIdle}
   180→	}
   181→}
   182→
   183→func transitionFromActive(event Event, ctx TransitionContext) TransitionResult {
   184→	switch event {
   185→	case EventTurnStart:
   186→		// Ctrl-C recovery: just continue.
   187→		// This is a degenerate case where the EndTurn is skipped after a in-turn commit.
   188→		// Either the agent crashed or the user interrupted it.
   189→		// We choose to continue, and defer condensation to the next TurnEnd or GitCommit.
   190→		return TransitionResult{
   191→			NewPhase: PhaseActive,
   192→			Actions:  []Action{ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
   193→		}
   194→	case EventTurnEnd:
   195→		return TransitionResult{
   196→			NewPhase: PhaseIdle,
   197→			Actions:  []Action{ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
   198→		}
   199→	case EventGitCommit:
   200→		if ctx.IsRebaseInProgress {
   201→			return TransitionResult{NewPhase: PhaseActive}
   202→		}
   203→		return TransitionResult{
   204→			NewPhase: PhaseActiveCommitted,
   205→			Actions:  []Action{ActionMigrateShadowBranch, ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
   206→		}
   207→	case EventSessionStart:
   208→		return TransitionResult{
   209→			NewPhase: PhaseActive,
   210→			Actions:  []Action{ActionWarnStaleSession},
   211→		}
   212→	case EventSessionStop:
   213→		return TransitionResult{
   214→			NewPhase: PhaseEnded,
   215→			Actions:  []Action{ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
   216→		}
   217→	default:
   218→		return TransitionResult{NewPhase: PhaseActive}
   219→	}
   220→}
   221→
   222→func transitionFromActiveCommitted(event Event, ctx TransitionContext) TransitionResult {
   223→	switch event {
   224→	case EventTurnStart:
   225→		// Ctrl-C recovery after commit.
   226→		// This is a degenerate case where the EndTurn is skipped after a in-turn commit.
   227→		// Either the agent crashed or the user interrupted it.
   228→		// We choose to continue, and defer condensation to the next TurnEnd or GitCommit.
   229→		return TransitionResult{
   230→			NewPhase: PhaseActive,
   231→			Actions:  []Action{ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
   232→		}
   233→	case EventTurnEnd:
   234→		return TransitionResult{
   235→			NewPhase: PhaseIdle,
   236→			Actions:  []Action{ActionCondense, ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
   237→		}
   238→	case EventGitCommit:
   239→		if ctx.IsRebaseInProgress {
   240→			return TransitionResult{NewPhase: PhaseActiveCommitted}
   241→		}
   242→		return TransitionResult{
   243→			NewPhase: PhaseActiveCommitted,
   244→			Actions:  []Action{ActionMigrateShadowBranch, ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
   245→		}
   246→	case EventSessionStart:
   247→		return TransitionResult{
   248→			NewPhase: PhaseActiveCommitted,
   249→			Actions:  []Action{ActionWarnStaleSession},
   250→		}
   251→	case EventSessionStop:
   252→		return TransitionResult{
   253→			NewPhase: PhaseEnded,
   254→			Actions:  []Action{ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
   255→		}
   256→	default:
   257→		return TransitionResult{NewPhase: PhaseActiveCommitted}
   258→	}
   259→}
   260→
   261→func transitionFromEnded(event Event, ctx TransitionContext) TransitionResult {
   262→	switch event {
   263→	case EventTurnStart:
   264→		return TransitionResult{
   265→			NewPhase: PhaseActive,
   266→			Actions:  []Action{ActionClearEndedAt, ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
   267→		}
   268→	case EventTurnEnd:
   269→		// Turn end while ended is a no-op.
   270→		return TransitionResult{NewPhase: PhaseEnded}
   271→	case EventGitCommit:
   272→		if ctx.IsRebaseInProgress {
   273→			return TransitionResult{NewPhase: PhaseEnded}
   274→		}
   275→		if ctx.HasFilesTouched {
   276→			return TransitionResult{
   277→				NewPhase: PhaseEnded,
   278→				Actions:  []Action{ActionCondenseIfFilesTouched, ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
   279→			}
   280→		}
   281→		return TransitionResult{
   282→			NewPhase: PhaseEnded,
   283→			Actions:  []Action{ActionDiscardIfNoFiles, ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
   284→		}
   285→	case EventSessionStart:
   286→		return TransitionResult{
   287→			NewPhase: PhaseIdle,
   288→			Actions:  []Action{ActionClearEndedAt},
   289→		}
   290→	case EventSessionStop:
   291→		// Already ended, no-op.
   292→		return TransitionResult{NewPhase: PhaseEnded}
   293→	default:
   294→		return TransitionResult{NewPhase: PhaseEnded}
   295→	}
   296→}
   297→
   298→// ApplyCommonActions applies the common (non-strategy-specific) actions from a
   299→// TransitionResult to the given State. It updates Phase, LastInteractionTime,
   300→// and EndedAt as indicated by the transition.
   301→//
   302→// Returns the subset of actions that require strategy-specific handling
   303→// (e.g., ActionCondense, ActionMigrateShadowBranch, ActionWarnStaleSession).
   304→// The caller is responsible for dispatching those.
   305→func ApplyCommonActions(state *State, result TransitionResult) []Action {
   306→	state.Phase = result.NewPhase
   307→
   308→	var remaining []Action
   309→	for _, action := range result.Actions {
   310→		switch action {
   311→		case ActionUpdateLastInteraction:
   312→			now := time.Now()
   313→			state.LastInteractionTime = &now
   314→		case ActionClearEndedAt:
   315→			state.EndedAt = nil
   316→		case ActionCondense, ActionCondenseIfFilesTouched, ActionDiscardIfNoFiles,
   317→			ActionMigrateShadowBranch, ActionWarnStaleSession:
   318→			// Strategy-specific actions — pass through to caller.
   319→			remaining = append(remaining, action)
   320→		}
   321→	}
   322→	return remaining
   323→}
   324→
   325→// MermaidDiagram generates a Mermaid state diagram from the transition table.
   326→// The diagram is derived by calling Transition() for representative context
   327→// variants per phase/event, so it stays in sync with the implementation.
   328→func MermaidDiagram() string {
   329→	var b strings.Builder
   330→	b.WriteString("stateDiagram-v2\n")
   331→
   332→	// State declarations with descriptions.
   333→	b.WriteString("    state \"IDLE\" as idle\n")
   334→	b.WriteString("    state \"ACTIVE\" as active\n")
   335→	b.WriteString("    state \"ACTIVE_COMMITTED\" as active_committed\n")
   336→	b.WriteString("    state \"ENDED\" as ended\n")
   337→	b.WriteString("\n")
   338→
   339→	// Context variants for GitCommit: rebase, files/no-files.
   340→	type contextVariant struct {
   341→		label string
   342→		ctx   TransitionContext
   343→	}
   344→
   345→	// For each phase/event, generate edges.
   346→	for _, phase := range allPhases {
   347→		for _, event := range allEvents {
   348→			var variants []contextVariant
   349→			switch {
   350→			case event == EventGitCommit && phase == PhaseEnded:
   351→				// HasFilesTouched only affects PhaseEnded transitions;
   352→				// other phases produce identical edges for both values.
   353→				variants = []contextVariant{
   354→					{"[files]", TransitionContext{HasFilesTouched: true}},
   355→					{"[no files]", TransitionContext{HasFilesTouched: false}},
   356→					{"[rebase]", TransitionContext{IsRebaseInProgress: true}},
   357→				}
   358→			case event == EventGitCommit:
   359→				variants = []contextVariant{
   360→					{"", TransitionContext{}},
   361→					{"[rebase]", TransitionContext{IsRebaseInProgress: true}},
   362→				}
   363→			default:
   364→				variants = []contextVariant{
   365→					{"", TransitionContext{}},
   366→				}
   367→			}
   368→
   369→			for _, v := range variants {
   370→				result := Transition(phase, event, v.ctx)
   371→
   372→				// Build edge label.
   373→				label := event.String()
   374→				if v.label != "" {
   375→					label += " " + v.label
   376→				}
   377→				if len(result.Actions) > 0 {
   378→					actionNames := make([]string, 0, len(result.Actions))
   379→					for _, a := range result.Actions {
   380→						actionNames = append(actionNames, a.String())
   381→					}
   382→					label += " / " + strings.Join(actionNames, ", ")
   383→				}
   384→
   385→				from := string(phase)
   386→				to := string(result.NewPhase)
   387→
   388→				fmt.Fprintf(&b, "    %s --> %s : %s\n", from, to, label)
   389→			}
   390→		}
   391→	}
   392→
   393→	return b.String()
   394→}
   395→

    14→func TestGetGitDirInPath_RegularRepo(t *testing.T) {
    15→	// Create a temp directory and initialize a real git repo
    16→	tmpDir := t.TempDir()
    17→
    18→	ctx := context.Background()
    19→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "init")
    20→	cmd.Dir = tmpDir
    21→	if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
    22→		t.Fatalf("failed to init git repo: %v", err)
    23→	}

   279→	cmd.Dir = mainRepo

   338→	if !isGitSequenceOperation() {
   339→		t.Error("isGitSequenceOperation() = false in worktree during rebase, want true")
   340→	}
   341→}

   343→func TestInstallGitHook_Idempotent(t *testing.T) {
   344→	// Create a temp directory and initialize a real git repo
   345→	tmpDir := t.TempDir()
   346→	t.Chdir(tmpDir)
   347→
   348→	ctx := context.Background()
   349→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "init")
   350→	cmd.Dir = tmpDir
   351→	if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
   352→		t.Fatalf("failed to init git repo: %v", err)
   353→	}
   354→
   355→	// Clear cache so paths resolve correctly
   356→	paths.ClearRepoRootCache()
   357→
   358→	// First install should install hooks
   359→	firstCount, err := InstallGitHook(true)
   360→	if err != nil {
   361→		t.Fatalf("First InstallGitHook() error = %v", err)
   362→	}
   363→	if firstCount == 0 {
   364→		t.Error("First InstallGitHook() should install hooks (count > 0)")
   365→	}
   366→
   367→	// Second install should return 0 (all hooks already up to date)
   368→	secondCount, err := InstallGitHook(true)
   369→	if err != nil {
   370→		t.Fatalf("Second InstallGitHook() error = %v", err)
   371→	}
   372→	if secondCount != 0 {
   373→		t.Errorf("Second InstallGitHook() returned %d, want 0 (hooks unchanged)", secondCount)
   374→	}
   375→}

   377→func TestRemoveGitHook_RemovesInstalledHooks(t *testing.T) {
   378→	// Create a temp directory and initialize a real git repo
   379→	tmpDir := t.TempDir()
   380→	t.Chdir(tmpDir)
   381→
   382→	ctx := context.Background()
   383→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "init")
   384→	cmd.Dir = tmpDir
   385→	if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
   386→		t.Fatalf("failed to init git repo: %v", err)
   387→	}
   388→
   389→	// Clear cache so paths resolve correctly
   390→	paths.ClearRepoRootCache()
   391→
   392→	// Install hooks first
   393→	installCount, err := InstallGitHook(true)
   394→	if err != nil {
   395→		t.Fatalf("InstallGitHook() error = %v", err)
   396→	}
   397→	if installCount == 0 {
   398→		t.Fatal("InstallGitHook() should install hooks")
   399→	}
   400→
   401→	// Verify hooks are installed
   402→	if !IsGitHookInstalled() {
   403→		t.Fatal("hooks should be installed before removal test")
   404→	}
   405→
   406→	// Remove hooks
   407→	removeCount, err := RemoveGitHook()
   408→	if err != nil {
   409→		t.Fatalf("RemoveGitHook() error = %v", err)
   410→	}
   411→	if removeCount != installCount {
   412→		t.Errorf("RemoveGitHook() returned %d, want %d (same as installed)", removeCount, installCount)
   413→	}
   414→
   415→	// Verify hooks are removed
   416→	if IsGitHookInstalled() {
   417→		t.Error("hooks should not be installed after removal")
   418→	}
   419→
   420→	// Verify hook files no longer exist
   421→	hooksDir := filepath.Join(tmpDir, ".git", "hooks")
   422→	for _, hookName := range gitHookNames {
   423→		hookPath := filepath.Join(hooksDir, hookName)
   424→		if _, err := os.Stat(hookPath); !os.IsNotExist(err) {
   425→			t.Errorf("hook file %s should not exist after removal", hookName)
   426→		}
   427→	}
   428→}

   511→	}

   513→	// Create a temp directory and initialize a real git repo

   516→

   518→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "init")
   519→	cmd.Dir = tmpDir

   521→		t.Fatalf("failed to init git repo: %v", err)
   522→	}
   523→
   524→	// Clear cache so paths resolve correctly
   525→	paths.ClearRepoRootCache()
   526→
   527→	// Install hooks first
   528→	_, err := InstallGitHook(true)
   529→	if err != nil {
   530→		t.Fatalf("InstallGitHook() error = %v", err)
   531→	}
   532→
   533→	// Remove write permissions from hooks directory to cause permission error
   534→	hooksDir := filepath.Join(tmpDir, ".git", "hooks")
   535→	if err := os.Chmod(hooksDir, 0o555); err != nil {
   536→		t.Fatalf("failed to change hooks dir permissions: %v", err)
   537→	}
   538→	// Restore permissions on cleanup
   539→	t.Cleanup(func() {
   540→		_ = os.Chmod(hooksDir, 0o755) //nolint:errcheck // Cleanup, best-effort
   541→	})
   542→
   543→	// Remove hooks should now fail with permission error
   544→	removed, err := RemoveGitHook()
   545→	if err == nil {
   546→		t.Fatal("RemoveGitHook() should return error when hooks cannot be deleted")
   547→	}
   548→	if removed != 0 {
   549→		t.Errorf("RemoveGitHook() removed %d hooks, expected 0 when all fail", removed)
   550→	}
   551→	if !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "failed to remove hooks") {
   552→		t.Errorf("error should mention 'failed to remove hooks', got: %v", err)
   553→	}
   554→}

    21→// TestPostCommit_ActiveSession_NoCondensation verifies that PostCommit on an
    22→// ACTIVE session transitions to ACTIVE_COMMITTED without condensing.
    23→// The shadow branch must be preserved because the session is still active.
    24→func TestPostCommit_ActiveSession_NoCondensation(t *testing.T) {
    25→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
    26→	t.Chdir(dir)
    27→
    28→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
    29→	require.NoError(t, err)
    30→
    31→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
    32→	sessionID := "test-postcommit-active"
    33→
    34→	// Initialize session and save a checkpoint so there is shadow branch content
    35→	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)
    36→
    37→	// Set phase to ACTIVE (simulating agent mid-turn)
    38→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
    39→	require.NoError(t, err)
    40→	state.Phase = session.PhaseActive
    41→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
    42→
    43→	// Create a commit WITH the Entire-Checkpoint trailer on the main branch
    44→	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "a1b2c3d4e5f6")
    45→
    46→	// Run PostCommit
    47→	err = s.PostCommit()
    48→	require.NoError(t, err)
    49→
    50→	// Verify phase transitioned to ACTIVE_COMMITTED
    51→	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
    52→	require.NoError(t, err)
    53→	require.NotNil(t, state)
    54→	assert.Equal(t, session.PhaseActiveCommitted, state.Phase,
    55→		"ACTIVE session should transition to ACTIVE_COMMITTED on GitCommit")
    56→
    57→	// Verify shadow branch is NOT deleted (session is still active).
    58→	// After PostCommit, BaseCommit is updated to new HEAD, so use the current state.
    59→	shadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
    60→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranch)
    61→	_, err = repo.Reference(refName, true)
    62→	assert.NoError(t, err,
    63→		"shadow branch should be preserved when session is still active")
    64→}
    65→
    66→// TestPostCommit_IdleSession_Condenses verifies that PostCommit on an IDLE
    67→// session condenses session data and cleans up the shadow branch.
    68→func TestPostCommit_IdleSession_Condenses(t *testing.T) {
    69→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
    70→	t.Chdir(dir)
    71→
    72→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
    73→	require.NoError(t, err)
    74→
    75→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
    76→	sessionID := "test-postcommit-idle"
    77→
    78→	// Initialize session and save a checkpoint so there is shadow branch content
    79→	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)
    80→
    81→	// Set phase to IDLE (agent turn finished, waiting for user)
    82→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
    83→	require.NoError(t, err)
    84→	state.Phase = session.PhaseIdle
    85→	state.LastInteractionTime = nil
    86→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
    87→
    88→	// Record shadow branch name before PostCommit
    89→	shadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
    90→
    91→	// Create a commit WITH the Entire-Checkpoint trailer
    92→	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "b2c3d4e5f6a1")
    93→
    94→	// Run PostCommit
    95→	err = s.PostCommit()
    96→	require.NoError(t, err)
    97→
    98→	// Verify condensation happened: the entire/sessions branch should exist
    99→	sessionsRef, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)
   100→	require.NoError(t, err, "entire/sessions branch should exist after condensation")
   101→	assert.NotNil(t, sessionsRef)
   102→
   103→	// Verify shadow branch IS deleted after condensation
   104→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranch)
   105→	_, err = repo.Reference(refName, true)
   106→	assert.Error(t, err,
   107→		"shadow branch should be deleted after condensation for IDLE session")
   108→}
   109→
   110→// TestPostCommit_RebaseDuringActive_SkipsTransition verifies that PostCommit
   111→// is a no-op during rebase operations, leaving the session phase unchanged.
   112→func TestPostCommit_RebaseDuringActive_SkipsTransition(t *testing.T) {
   113→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
   114→	t.Chdir(dir)
   115→
   116→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
   117→	require.NoError(t, err)
   118→
   119→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
   120→	sessionID := "test-postcommit-rebase"
   121→
   122→	// Initialize session and save a checkpoint
   123→	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)
   124→
   125→	// Set phase to ACTIVE
   126→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   127→	require.NoError(t, err)
   128→	state.Phase = session.PhaseActive
   129→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
   130→
   131→	// Capture shadow branch name BEFORE any state changes
   132→	shadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   133→	originalStepCount := state.StepCount
   134→
   135→	// Simulate rebase in progress by creating .git/rebase-merge/ directory
   136→	gitDir := filepath.Join(dir, ".git")
   137→	rebaseMergeDir := filepath.Join(gitDir, "rebase-merge")
   138→	require.NoError(t, os.MkdirAll(rebaseMergeDir, 0o755))
   139→	defer os.RemoveAll(rebaseMergeDir)
   140→
   141→	// Create a commit WITH the checkpoint trailer
   142→	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "c3d4e5f6a1b2")
   143→
   144→	// Run PostCommit
   145→	err = s.PostCommit()
   146→	require.NoError(t, err)
   147→
   148→	// Verify phase stayed ACTIVE (no transition during rebase)
   149→	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   150→	require.NoError(t, err)
   151→	require.NotNil(t, state)
   152→	assert.Equal(t, session.PhaseActive, state.Phase,
   153→		"session should stay ACTIVE during rebase (no transition)")
   154→
   155→	// Verify StepCount was NOT reset (no condensation happened)
   156→	assert.Equal(t, originalStepCount, state.StepCount,
   157→		"StepCount should be unchanged - no condensation during rebase")
   158→
   159→	// Verify NO condensation happened (entire/sessions branch should not exist)
   160→	_, err = repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)
   161→	require.Error(t, err,
   162→		"entire/sessions branch should NOT exist - no condensation during rebase")
   163→
   164→	// Verify shadow branch still exists (not cleaned up during rebase)
   165→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranch)
   166→	_, err = repo.Reference(refName, true)
   167→	assert.NoError(t, err,
   168→		"shadow branch should be preserved during rebase")
   169→}
   170→
   171→// TestPostCommit_ShadowBranch_PreservedWhenActiveSessionExists verifies that
   172→// the shadow branch is preserved when ANY session on it is still active,
   173→// even if another session on the same branch is IDLE and gets condensed.
   174→func TestPostCommit_ShadowBranch_PreservedWhenActiveSessionExists(t *testing.T) {
   175→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
   176→	t.Chdir(dir)
   177→
   178→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
   179→	require.NoError(t, err)
   180→
   181→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
   182→	idleSessionID := "test-postcommit-idle-multi"
   183→	activeSessionID := "test-postcommit-active-multi"
   184→
   185→	// Initialize the idle session with a checkpoint
   186→	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, idleSessionID)
   187→
   188→	// Get worktree path and base commit from the idle session
   189→	idleState, err := s.loadSessionState(idleSessionID)
   190→	require.NoError(t, err)
   191→	worktreePath := idleState.WorktreePath
   192→	baseCommit := idleState.BaseCommit
   193→	worktreeID := idleState.WorktreeID
   194→
   195→	// Set idle session to IDLE phase
   196→	idleState.Phase = session.PhaseIdle
   197→	idleState.LastInteractionTime = nil
   198→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(idleState))
   199→
   200→	// Create a second session with the SAME base commit and worktree (concurrent session)
   201→	// Save the active session with ACTIVE phase and some checkpoints
   202→	now := time.Now()
   203→	activeState := &SessionState{
   204→		SessionID:           activeSessionID,
   205→		BaseCommit:          baseCommit,
   206→		WorktreePath:        worktreePath,
   207→		WorktreeID:          worktreeID,
   208→		StartedAt:           now,
   209→		Phase:               session.PhaseActive,
   210→		LastInteractionTime: &now,
   211→		StepCount:           1,
   212→	}
   213→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(activeState))
   214→
   215→	// Create a commit WITH the checkpoint trailer
   216→	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "d4e5f6a1b2c3")
   217→
   218→	// Run PostCommit
   219→	err = s.PostCommit()
   220→	require.NoError(t, err)
   221→
   222→	// Verify the ACTIVE session's phase is now ACTIVE_COMMITTED
   223→	activeState, err = s.loadSessionState(activeSessionID)
   224→	require.NoError(t, err)
   225→	assert.Equal(t, session.PhaseActiveCommitted, activeState.Phase,
   226→		"ACTIVE session should transition to ACTIVE_COMMITTED on GitCommit")
   227→
   228→	// Verify the IDLE session actually condensed (entire/sessions branch should exist)
   229→	idleState, err = s.loadSessionState(idleSessionID)
   230→	require.NoError(t, err)
   231→	sessionsRef, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)
   232→	require.NoError(t, err, "entire/sessions branch should exist after IDLE session condensation")
   233→	require.NotNil(t, sessionsRef)
   234→
   235→	// Verify IDLE session's StepCount was reset by condensation
   236→	assert.Equal(t, 0, idleState.StepCount,
   237→		"IDLE session StepCount should be reset after condensation")
   238→
   239→	// Verify shadow branch is NOT deleted because the ACTIVE session still needs it.
   240→	// After PostCommit, BaseCommit is updated to new HEAD via migration.
   241→	newShadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(activeState.BaseCommit, activeState.WorktreeID)
   242→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(newShadowBranch)
   243→	_, err = repo.Reference(refName, true)
   244→	assert.NoError(t, err,
   245→		"shadow branch should be preserved when an active session still exists on it")
   246→}
   247→
   248→// TestPostCommit_CondensationFailure_PreservesShadowBranch verifies that when
   249→// condensation fails (corrupted shadow branch), BaseCommit is NOT updated.
   250→func TestPostCommit_CondensationFailure_PreservesShadowBranch(t *testing.T) {
   251→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
   252→	t.Chdir(dir)
   253→
   254→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
   255→	require.NoError(t, err)
   256→
   257→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
   258→	sessionID := "test-postcommit-condense-fail-idle"
   259→
   260→	// Initialize session and save a checkpoint
   261→	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)
   262→
   263→	// Set phase to IDLE
   264→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   265→	require.NoError(t, err)
   266→	state.Phase = session.PhaseIdle
   267→	state.LastInteractionTime = nil
   268→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
   269→
   270→	// Record original BaseCommit and StepCount before corruption
   271→	originalBaseCommit := state.BaseCommit
   272→	originalStepCount := state.StepCount
   273→
   274→	// Corrupt shadow branch by pointing it at ZeroHash
   275→	shadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   276→	corruptRef := plumbing.NewHashReference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranch), plumbing.ZeroHash)
   277→	require.NoError(t, repo.Storer.SetReference(corruptRef))
   278→
   279→	// Create a commit with the checkpoint trailer
   280→	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "e5f6a1b2c3d4")
   281→
   282→	// Run PostCommit
   283→	err = s.PostCommit()
   284→	require.NoError(t, err, "PostCommit should not return error even when condensation fails")
   285→
   286→	// Verify BaseCommit was NOT updated (condensation failed)
   287→	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   288→	require.NoError(t, err)
   289→	assert.Equal(t, originalBaseCommit, state.BaseCommit,
   290→		"BaseCommit should NOT be updated when condensation fails")
   291→	assert.Equal(t, originalStepCount, state.StepCount,
   292→		"StepCount should NOT be reset when condensation fails")
   293→
   294→	// Verify entire/sessions branch does NOT exist (condensation failed)
   295→	_, err = repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)
   296→	require.Error(t, err,
   297→		"entire/sessions branch should NOT exist when condensation fails")
   298→
   299→	// Phase transition still applies even when condensation fails
   300→	assert.Equal(t, session.PhaseIdle, state.Phase,
   301→		"phase should remain IDLE when condensation fails")
   302→}
   303→
   304→// TestPostCommit_IdleSession_NoNewContent_UpdatesBaseCommit verifies that when
   305→// an IDLE session has no new transcript content since last condensation,
   306→// PostCommit skips condensation but still updates BaseCommit.
   307→func TestPostCommit_IdleSession_NoNewContent_UpdatesBaseCommit(t *testing.T) {
   308→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
   309→	t.Chdir(dir)
   310→
   311→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
   312→	require.NoError(t, err)
   313→
   314→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
   315→	sessionID := "test-postcommit-idle-no-content"
   316→
   317→	// Initialize session and save a checkpoint
   318→	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)
   319→
   320→	// Set phase to IDLE with CheckpointTranscriptStart matching transcript length (2 lines)
   321→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   322→	require.NoError(t, err)
   323→	state.Phase = session.PhaseIdle
   324→	state.LastInteractionTime = nil
   325→	state.CheckpointTranscriptStart = 2 // Transcript has exactly 2 lines
   326→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
   327→
   328→	// Record shadow branch name
   329→	shadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   330→	originalStepCount := state.StepCount
   331→
   332→	// Create a commit with the checkpoint trailer
   333→	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "f6a1b2c3d4e5")
   334→
   335→	// Get new HEAD hash for comparison
   336→	head, err := repo.Head()
   337→	require.NoError(t, err)
   338→	newHeadHash := head.Hash().String()
   339→
   340→	// Run PostCommit
   341→	err = s.PostCommit()
   342→	require.NoError(t, err)
   343→
   344→	// Verify BaseCommit was updated to new HEAD
   345→	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   346→	require.NoError(t, err)
   347→	assert.Equal(t, newHeadHash, state.BaseCommit,
   348→		"BaseCommit should be updated to new HEAD when no new content")
   349→
   350→	// Shadow branch should still exist (not deleted, no condensation)
   351→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranch)
   352→	_, err = repo.Reference(refName, true)
   353→	require.NoError(t, err,
   354→		"shadow branch should still exist when no condensation happened")
   355→
   356→	// entire/sessions branch should NOT exist (no condensation)
   357→	_, err = repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)
   358→	require.Error(t, err,
   359→		"entire/sessions branch should NOT exist when no condensation happened")
   360→
   361→	// StepCount should be unchanged
   362→	assert.Equal(t, originalStepCount, state.StepCount,
   363→		"StepCount should be unchanged when no condensation happened")
   364→}

   366→// TestPostCommit_EndedSession_FilesTouched_Condenses verifies that an ENDED
   367→// session with files touched and new content condenses on commit.
   368→func TestPostCommit_EndedSession_FilesTouched_Condenses(t *testing.T) {
   369→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
   370→	t.Chdir(dir)
   371→
   372→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
   373→	require.NoError(t, err)
   374→
   375→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
   376→	sessionID := "test-postcommit-ended-condenses"
   377→
   378→	// Initialize session and save a checkpoint
   379→	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)
   380→
   381→	// Set phase to ENDED with files touched
   382→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   383→	require.NoError(t, err)
   384→	now := time.Now()
   385→	state.Phase = session.PhaseEnded
   386→	state.EndedAt = &now
   387→	state.FilesTouched = []string{"test.txt"}
   388→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
   389→
   390→	// Record shadow branch name
   391→	shadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   392→
   393→	// Create a commit with the checkpoint trailer
   394→	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "a1b2c3d4e5f7")
   395→
   396→	// Run PostCommit
   397→	err = s.PostCommit()
   398→	require.NoError(t, err)
   399→
   400→	// Verify entire/sessions branch exists (condensation happened)
   401→	sessionsRef, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)
   402→	require.NoError(t, err, "entire/sessions branch should exist after condensation")
   403→	assert.NotNil(t, sessionsRef)
   404→
   405→	// Verify old shadow branch is deleted after condensation
   406→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranch)
   407→	_, err = repo.Reference(refName, true)
   408→	require.Error(t, err,
   409→		"shadow branch should be deleted after condensation for ENDED session")
   410→
   411→	// Verify StepCount was reset by condensation
   412→	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   413→	require.NoError(t, err)
   414→	assert.Equal(t, 0, state.StepCount,
   415→		"StepCount should be reset after condensation")
   416→
   417→	// Phase stays ENDED
   418→	assert.Equal(t, session.PhaseEnded, state.Phase,
   419→		"ENDED session should stay ENDED after condensation")
   420→}

   422→// TestPostCommit_EndedSession_FilesTouched_NoNewContent verifies that an ENDED
   423→// session with files touched but no new transcript content skips condensation
   424→// and updates BaseCommit via fallthrough.
   425→func TestPostCommit_EndedSession_FilesTouched_NoNewContent(t *testing.T) {
   426→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
   427→	t.Chdir(dir)
   428→
   429→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
   430→	require.NoError(t, err)
   431→
   432→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
   433→	sessionID := "test-postcommit-ended-no-content"
   434→
   435→	// Initialize session and save a checkpoint
   436→	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)
   437→
   438→	// Set phase to ENDED with files touched but no new content
   439→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   440→	require.NoError(t, err)
   441→	now := time.Now()
   442→	state.Phase = session.PhaseEnded
   443→	state.EndedAt = &now
   444→	state.FilesTouched = []string{"test.txt"}
   445→	state.CheckpointTranscriptStart = 2 // Transcript has exactly 2 lines
   446→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
   447→
   448→	// Record shadow branch name and original StepCount
   449→	shadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   450→	originalStepCount := state.StepCount
   451→
   452→	// Create a commit with the checkpoint trailer
   453→	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "b2c3d4e5f6a2")
   454→
   455→	// Get new HEAD hash
   456→	head, err := repo.Head()
   457→	require.NoError(t, err)
   458→	newHeadHash := head.Hash().String()
   459→
   460→	// Run PostCommit
   461→	err = s.PostCommit()
   462→	require.NoError(t, err)
   463→
   464→	// Verify entire/sessions branch does NOT exist (no condensation)
   465→	_, err = repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)
   466→	require.Error(t, err,
   467→		"entire/sessions branch should NOT exist when no new content")
   468→
   469→	// Shadow branch should still exist
   470→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranch)
   471→	_, err = repo.Reference(refName, true)
   472→	require.NoError(t, err,
   473→		"shadow branch should still exist when no condensation happened")
   474→
   475→	// BaseCommit should be updated to new HEAD via fallthrough
   476→	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   477→	require.NoError(t, err)
   478→	assert.Equal(t, newHeadHash, state.BaseCommit,
   479→		"BaseCommit should be updated to new HEAD via fallthrough")
   480→
   481→	// StepCount unchanged
   482→	assert.Equal(t, originalStepCount, state.StepCount,
   483→		"StepCount should be unchanged when no condensation happened")
   484→}

   486→// TestPostCommit_EndedSession_NoFilesTouched_Discards verifies that an ENDED
   487→// session with no files touched takes the discard path, updating BaseCommit.
   488→func TestPostCommit_EndedSession_NoFilesTouched_Discards(t *testing.T) {
   489→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
   490→	t.Chdir(dir)
   491→
   492→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
   493→	require.NoError(t, err)
   494→
   495→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
   496→	sessionID := "test-postcommit-ended-discard"
   497→
   498→	// Initialize session and save a checkpoint
   499→	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)
   500→
   501→	// Set phase to ENDED with no files touched
   502→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   503→	require.NoError(t, err)
   504→	now := time.Now()
   505→	state.Phase = session.PhaseEnded
   506→	state.EndedAt = &now
   507→	state.FilesTouched = nil // No files touched
   508→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
   509→
   510→	// Record original StepCount
   511→	originalStepCount := state.StepCount
   512→
   513→	// Create a commit with the checkpoint trailer
   514→	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "c3d4e5f6a1b3")
   515→
   516→	// Get new HEAD hash
   517→	head, err := repo.Head()
   518→	require.NoError(t, err)
   519→	newHeadHash := head.Hash().String()
   520→
   521→	// Run PostCommit
   522→	err = s.PostCommit()
   523→	require.NoError(t, err)
   524→
   525→	// Verify entire/sessions branch does NOT exist (no condensation for discard path)
   526→	_, err = repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)
   527→	require.Error(t, err,
   528→		"entire/sessions branch should NOT exist for discard path")
   529→
   530→	// BaseCommit should be updated to new HEAD
   531→	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   532→	require.NoError(t, err)
   533→	assert.Equal(t, newHeadHash, state.BaseCommit,
   534→		"BaseCommit should be updated to new HEAD on discard path")
   535→
   536→	// StepCount unchanged
   537→	assert.Equal(t, originalStepCount, state.StepCount,
   538→		"StepCount should be unchanged on discard path")
   539→
   540→	// Phase stays ENDED
   541→	assert.Equal(t, session.PhaseEnded, state.Phase,
   542→		"ENDED session should stay ENDED on discard path")
   543→}
   544→
   545→// TestPostCommit_ActiveCommitted_MigratesShadowBranch verifies that an
   546→// ACTIVE_COMMITTED session receiving another commit migrates the shadow branch
   547→// to the new HEAD and stays in ACTIVE_COMMITTED.
   548→func TestPostCommit_ActiveCommitted_MigratesShadowBranch(t *testing.T) {
   549→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
   550→	t.Chdir(dir)
   551→
   552→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
   553→	require.NoError(t, err)
   554→
   555→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
   556→	sessionID := "test-postcommit-ac-migrate"
   557→
   558→	// Initialize session and save a checkpoint
   559→	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)
   560→
   561→	// Set phase to ACTIVE_COMMITTED
   562→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   563→	require.NoError(t, err)
   564→	state.Phase = session.PhaseActiveCommitted
   565→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
   566→
   567→	// Record original shadow branch name and BaseCommit
   568→	originalShadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   569→	originalStepCount := state.StepCount
   570→
   571→	// Create a commit with the checkpoint trailer
   572→	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "d4e5f6a1b2c4")
   573→
   574→	// Run PostCommit
   575→	err = s.PostCommit()
   576→	require.NoError(t, err)
   577→
   578→	// Verify phase stays ACTIVE_COMMITTED
   579→	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   580→	require.NoError(t, err)
   581→	assert.Equal(t, session.PhaseActiveCommitted, state.Phase,
   582→		"ACTIVE_COMMITTED session should stay ACTIVE_COMMITTED on subsequent commit")
   583→
   584→	// Verify BaseCommit updated to new HEAD
   585→	head, err := repo.Head()
   586→	require.NoError(t, err)
   587→	assert.Equal(t, head.Hash().String(), state.BaseCommit,
   588→		"BaseCommit should be updated to new HEAD after migration")
   589→
   590→	// Verify new shadow branch exists at new HEAD hash
   591→	newShadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   592→	newRefName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(newShadowBranch)
   593→	_, err = repo.Reference(newRefName, true)
   594→	require.NoError(t, err,
   595→		"new shadow branch should exist after migration")
   596→
   597→	// Verify original shadow branch no longer exists (was migrated/renamed)
   598→	oldRefName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(originalShadowBranch)
   599→	_, err = repo.Reference(oldRefName, true)
   600→	require.Error(t, err,
   601→		"original shadow branch should no longer exist after migration")
   602→
   603→	// StepCount unchanged (no condensation)
   604→	assert.Equal(t, originalStepCount, state.StepCount,
   605→		"StepCount should be unchanged - no condensation for ACTIVE_COMMITTED")
   606→
   607→	// entire/sessions branch should NOT exist (no condensation)
   608→	_, err = repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)
   609→	require.Error(t, err,
   610→		"entire/sessions branch should NOT exist - no condensation for ACTIVE_COMMITTED")
   611→}
   612→
   613→// TestPostCommit_CondensationFailure_EndedSession_PreservesShadowBranch verifies
   614→// that when condensation fails for an ENDED session with files touched,
   615→// BaseCommit is preserved (not updated).
   616→func TestPostCommit_CondensationFailure_EndedSession_PreservesShadowBranch(t *testing.T) {
   617→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
   618→	t.Chdir(dir)
   619→
   620→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
   621→	require.NoError(t, err)
   622→
   623→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
   624→	sessionID := "test-postcommit-condense-fail-ended"
   625→
   626→	// Initialize session and save a checkpoint
   627→	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)
   628→
   629→	// Set phase to ENDED with files touched
   630→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   631→	require.NoError(t, err)
   632→	now := time.Now()
   633→	state.Phase = session.PhaseEnded
   634→	state.EndedAt = &now
   635→	state.FilesTouched = []string{"test.txt"}
   636→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
   637→
   638→	// Record original BaseCommit and StepCount
   639→	originalBaseCommit := state.BaseCommit
   640→	originalStepCount := state.StepCount
   641→
   642→	// Corrupt shadow branch by pointing it at ZeroHash
   643→	shadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   644→	corruptRef := plumbing.NewHashReference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranch), plumbing.ZeroHash)
   645→	require.NoError(t, repo.Storer.SetReference(corruptRef))
   646→
   647→	// Create a commit with the checkpoint trailer
   648→	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "e5f6a1b2c3d5")
   649→
   650→	// Run PostCommit
   651→	err = s.PostCommit()
   652→	require.NoError(t, err, "PostCommit should not return error even when condensation fails")
   653→
   654→	// Verify BaseCommit was NOT updated (condensation failed)
   655→	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   656→	require.NoError(t, err)
   657→	assert.Equal(t, originalBaseCommit, state.BaseCommit,
   658→		"BaseCommit should NOT be updated when condensation fails for ENDED session")
   659→	assert.Equal(t, originalStepCount, state.StepCount,
   660→		"StepCount should NOT be reset when condensation fails for ENDED session")
   661→
   662→	// Verify entire/sessions branch does NOT exist (condensation failed)
   663→	_, err = repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)
   664→	require.Error(t, err,
   665→		"entire/sessions branch should NOT exist when condensation fails")
   666→
   667→	// Phase stays ENDED
   668→	assert.Equal(t, session.PhaseEnded, state.Phase,
   669→		"ENDED session should stay ENDED when condensation fails")
   670→}

   672→// TestPostCommit_ActiveSession_SetsPendingCheckpointID verifies that PostCommit
   673→// stores PendingCheckpointID when transitioning ACTIVE → ACTIVE_COMMITTED.
   674→// This ensures HandleTurnEnd can reuse the same checkpoint ID that's in the
   675→// commit trailer, rather than generating a mismatched one.
   676→func TestPostCommit_ActiveSession_SetsPendingCheckpointID(t *testing.T) {
   677→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
   678→	t.Chdir(dir)
   679→
   680→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
   681→	require.NoError(t, err)
   682→
   683→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
   684→	sessionID := "test-postcommit-pending-cpid"
   685→
   686→	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)
   687→
   688→	// Set phase to ACTIVE (agent mid-turn)
   689→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   690→	require.NoError(t, err)
   691→	state.Phase = session.PhaseActive
   692→	state.PendingCheckpointID = "" // Ensure it starts empty
   693→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
   694→
   695→	// Create a commit with a known checkpoint ID
   696→	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "a1b2c3d4e5f6")
   697→
   698→	// Run PostCommit
   699→	err = s.PostCommit()
   700→	require.NoError(t, err)
   701→
   702→	// Verify phase transitioned to ACTIVE_COMMITTED
   703→	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   704→	require.NoError(t, err)
   705→	require.Equal(t, session.PhaseActiveCommitted, state.Phase)
   706→
   707→	// Verify PendingCheckpointID was stored from the commit trailer
   708→	assert.Equal(t, "a1b2c3d4e5f6", state.PendingCheckpointID,
   709→		"PendingCheckpointID should be set to the commit's checkpoint ID for deferred condensation")
   710→}
   711→
   712→// TestTurnEnd_ActiveCommitted_ReusesCheckpointID verifies that HandleTurnEnd
   713→// uses PendingCheckpointID (set by PostCommit) rather than generating a new one.
   714→// This ensures the condensed metadata matches the commit trailer.
   715→func TestTurnEnd_ActiveCommitted_ReusesCheckpointID(t *testing.T) {
   716→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
   717→	t.Chdir(dir)
   718→
   719→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
   720→	require.NoError(t, err)
   721→
   722→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
   723→	sessionID := "test-turnend-reuses-cpid"
   724→
   725→	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)
   726→
   727→	// Simulate PostCommit: transition to ACTIVE_COMMITTED with PendingCheckpointID
   728→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   729→	require.NoError(t, err)
   730→	state.Phase = session.PhaseActive
   731→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
   732→
   733→	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "d4e5f6a1b2c3")
   734→
   735→	err = s.PostCommit()
   736→	require.NoError(t, err)
   737→
   738→	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   739→	require.NoError(t, err)
   740→	require.Equal(t, "d4e5f6a1b2c3", state.PendingCheckpointID)
   741→
   742→	// Run TurnEnd
   743→	result := session.Transition(state.Phase, session.EventTurnEnd, session.TransitionContext{})
   744→	remaining := session.ApplyCommonActions(state, result)
   745→
   746→	err = s.HandleTurnEnd(state, remaining)
   747→	require.NoError(t, err)
   748→
   749→	// Verify the condensed checkpoint ID matches the commit trailer
   750→	// The LastCheckpointID is set by condenseAndUpdateState on success
   751→	assert.Equal(t, id.CheckpointID("d4e5f6a1b2c3"), state.LastCheckpointID,
   752→		"condensation should use PendingCheckpointID, not generate a new one")
   753→}
   754→
   755→// TestTurnEnd_ConcurrentSession_PreservesShadowBranch verifies that
   756→// HandleTurnEnd does NOT delete the shadow branch when another active
   757→// session shares it.
   758→func TestTurnEnd_ConcurrentSession_PreservesShadowBranch(t *testing.T) {
   759→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
   760→	t.Chdir(dir)
   761→
   762→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
   763→	require.NoError(t, err)
   764→
   765→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
   766→	sessionID1 := "test-turnend-concurrent-1"
   767→	sessionID2 := "test-turnend-concurrent-2"
   768→
   769→	// Initialize first session and save a checkpoint
   770→	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID1)
   771→
   772→	// Get worktree info from first session
   773→	state1, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID1)
   774→	require.NoError(t, err)
   775→	worktreePath := state1.WorktreePath
   776→	baseCommit := state1.BaseCommit
   777→	worktreeID := state1.WorktreeID
   778→
   779→	// Transition first session through PostCommit to ACTIVE_COMMITTED
   780→	state1.Phase = session.PhaseActive
   781→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state1))
   782→
   783→	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "e5f6a1b2c3d4")
   784→
   785→	err = s.PostCommit()
   786→	require.NoError(t, err)
   787→
   788→	state1, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID1)
   789→	require.NoError(t, err)
   790→	require.Equal(t, session.PhaseActiveCommitted, state1.Phase)
   791→
   792→	// Create a second session with the SAME base commit and worktree (concurrent)
   793→	now := time.Now()
   794→	state2 := &SessionState{
   795→		SessionID:           sessionID2,
   796→		BaseCommit:          state1.BaseCommit, // Same base commit (post-migration)
   797→		WorktreePath:        worktreePath,
   798→		WorktreeID:          worktreeID,
   799→		StartedAt:           now,
   800→		Phase:               session.PhaseActive,
   801→		LastInteractionTime: &now,
   802→		StepCount:           1,
   803→	}
   804→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state2))
   805→
   806→	// Record shadow branch name (shared by both sessions)
   807→	shadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state1.BaseCommit, state1.WorktreeID)
   808→
   809→	// First session ends its turn — should NOT delete shadow branch
   810→	result := session.Transition(state1.Phase, session.EventTurnEnd, session.TransitionContext{})
   811→	remaining := session.ApplyCommonActions(state1, result)
   812→
   813→	err = s.HandleTurnEnd(state1, remaining)
   814→	require.NoError(t, err)
   815→
   816→	// Shadow branch at the pre-condensation BaseCommit should be preserved
   817→	// because session2 is still active on it.
   818→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranch)
   819→	_, err = repo.Reference(refName, true)
   820→	require.NoError(t, err,
   821→		"shadow branch should be preserved when another active session shares it")
   822→
   823→	// Condensation still succeeded for session1
   824→	assert.Equal(t, 0, state1.StepCount,
   825→		"StepCount should be reset after condensation")
   826→	assert.Equal(t, session.PhaseIdle, state1.Phase,
   827→		"first session should be IDLE after turn end")
   828→
   829→	// Second session is still active and unaffected
   830→	state2, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID2)
   831→	require.NoError(t, err)
   832→	assert.Equal(t, session.PhaseActive, state2.Phase,
   833→		"second session should still be ACTIVE")
   834→	_ = baseCommit // used for documentation
   835→}
   836→
   837→// TestTurnEnd_ActiveCommitted_CondensesSession verifies that HandleTurnEnd
   838→// with ActionCondense (from ACTIVE_COMMITTED → IDLE) condenses the session
   839→// to entire/sessions and cleans up the shadow branch.
   840→func TestTurnEnd_ActiveCommitted_CondensesSession(t *testing.T) {
   841→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
   842→	t.Chdir(dir)
   843→
   844→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
   845→	require.NoError(t, err)
   846→
   847→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
   848→	sessionID := "test-turnend-condenses"
   849→
   850→	// Initialize session and save a checkpoint so there is shadow branch content
   851→	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)
   852→
   853→	// Simulate PostCommit: create a commit with trailer and transition to ACTIVE_COMMITTED
   854→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   855→	require.NoError(t, err)
   856→	state.Phase = session.PhaseActive
   857→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
   858→
   859→	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "a1b2c3d4e5f6")
   860→
   861→	// Run PostCommit so phase transitions to ACTIVE_COMMITTED and PendingCheckpointID is set
   862→	err = s.PostCommit()
   863→	require.NoError(t, err)
   864→
   865→	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   866→	require.NoError(t, err)
   867→	require.Equal(t, session.PhaseActiveCommitted, state.Phase)
   868→
   869→	// Now simulate the TurnEnd transition that the handler dispatches
   870→	result := session.Transition(state.Phase, session.EventTurnEnd, session.TransitionContext{})
   871→	remaining := session.ApplyCommonActions(state, result)
   872→
   873→	// Verify the state machine emits ActionCondense
   874→	require.Contains(t, remaining, session.ActionCondense,
   875→		"ACTIVE_COMMITTED + TurnEnd should emit ActionCondense")
   876→
   877→	// Record shadow branch name BEFORE HandleTurnEnd (BaseCommit may change)
   878→	shadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   879→
   880→	// Call HandleTurnEnd with the remaining actions
   881→	err = s.HandleTurnEnd(state, remaining)
   882→	require.NoError(t, err)
   883→
   884→	// Verify condensation happened: entire/sessions branch should exist
   885→	sessionsRef, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)
   886→	require.NoError(t, err, "entire/sessions branch should exist after turn-end condensation")
   887→	assert.NotNil(t, sessionsRef)
   888→
   889→	// Verify shadow branch IS deleted after condensation
   890→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranch)
   891→	_, err = repo.Reference(refName, true)
   892→	require.Error(t, err,
   893→		"shadow branch should be deleted after turn-end condensation")
   894→
   895→	// Verify StepCount was reset by condensation
   896→	assert.Equal(t, 0, state.StepCount,
   897→		"StepCount should be reset after condensation")
   898→
   899→	// Verify phase is IDLE (set by ApplyCommonActions above)
   900→	assert.Equal(t, session.PhaseIdle, state.Phase,
   901→		"phase should be IDLE after TurnEnd")
   902→}
   903→
   904→// TestTurnEnd_ActiveCommitted_CondensationFailure_PreservesShadowBranch verifies
   905→// that when HandleTurnEnd condensation fails, BaseCommit is NOT updated and
   906→// the shadow branch is preserved.
   907→func TestTurnEnd_ActiveCommitted_CondensationFailure_PreservesShadowBranch(t *testing.T) {
   908→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
   909→	t.Chdir(dir)
   910→
   911→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
   912→	require.NoError(t, err)
   913→
   914→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
   915→	sessionID := "test-turnend-condense-fail"
   916→
   917→	// Initialize session and save a checkpoint
   918→	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)
   919→
   920→	// Simulate PostCommit: transition to ACTIVE_COMMITTED
   921→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   922→	require.NoError(t, err)
   923→	state.Phase = session.PhaseActive
   924→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
   925→
   926→	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "b2c3d4e5f6a1")
   927→
   928→	err = s.PostCommit()
   929→	require.NoError(t, err)
   930→
   931→	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   932→	require.NoError(t, err)
   933→	require.Equal(t, session.PhaseActiveCommitted, state.Phase)
   934→
   935→	// Record original BaseCommit before corruption
   936→	originalBaseCommit := state.BaseCommit
   937→	originalStepCount := state.StepCount
   938→
   939→	// Corrupt shadow branch by pointing it at ZeroHash
   940→	shadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   941→	corruptRef := plumbing.NewHashReference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranch), plumbing.ZeroHash)
   942→	require.NoError(t, repo.Storer.SetReference(corruptRef))
   943→
   944→	// Run the transition
   945→	result := session.Transition(state.Phase, session.EventTurnEnd, session.TransitionContext{})
   946→	remaining := session.ApplyCommonActions(state, result)
   947→
   948→	// Call HandleTurnEnd — condensation should fail silently
   949→	err = s.HandleTurnEnd(state, remaining)
   950→	require.NoError(t, err, "HandleTurnEnd should not return error even when condensation fails")
   951→
   952→	// BaseCommit should NOT be updated (condensation failed)
   953→	assert.Equal(t, originalBaseCommit, state.BaseCommit,
   954→		"BaseCommit should NOT be updated when condensation fails")
   955→	assert.Equal(t, originalStepCount, state.StepCount,
   956→		"StepCount should NOT be reset when condensation fails")
   957→
   958→	// entire/sessions branch should NOT exist (condensation failed)
   959→	_, err = repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)
   960→	assert.Error(t, err,
   961→		"entire/sessions branch should NOT exist when condensation fails")
   962→}

   964→// TestTurnEnd_Active_NoActions verifies that HandleTurnEnd with no actions
   965→// is a no-op (normal ACTIVE → IDLE transition has no strategy-specific actions).
   966→func TestTurnEnd_Active_NoActions(t *testing.T) {
   967→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
   968→	t.Chdir(dir)
   969→
   970→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
   971→	require.NoError(t, err)
   972→
   973→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
   974→	sessionID := "test-turnend-no-actions"
   975→
   976→	// Initialize session and save a checkpoint
   977→	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)
   978→
   979→	// Set phase to ACTIVE (normal turn, no commit during turn)
   980→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   981→	require.NoError(t, err)
   982→	state.Phase = session.PhaseActive
   983→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
   984→
   985→	originalBaseCommit := state.BaseCommit
   986→	originalStepCount := state.StepCount
   987→
   988→	// ACTIVE + TurnEnd → IDLE with no strategy-specific actions
   989→	result := session.Transition(state.Phase, session.EventTurnEnd, session.TransitionContext{})
   990→	remaining := session.ApplyCommonActions(state, result)
   991→
   992→	// Verify no strategy-specific actions for ACTIVE → IDLE
   993→	assert.Empty(t, remaining,
   994→		"ACTIVE + TurnEnd should not emit strategy-specific actions")
   995→
   996→	// Call HandleTurnEnd with empty actions — should be a no-op
   997→	err = s.HandleTurnEnd(state, remaining)
   998→	require.NoError(t, err)
   999→
  1000→	// Verify state is unchanged
  1001→	assert.Equal(t, originalBaseCommit, state.BaseCommit,
  1002→		"BaseCommit should be unchanged for no-op turn end")
  1003→	assert.Equal(t, originalStepCount, state.StepCount,
  1004→		"StepCount should be unchanged for no-op turn end")
  1005→
  1006→	// Shadow branch should still exist (not cleaned up)
  1007→	shadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(originalBaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
  1008→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranch)
  1009→	_, err = repo.Reference(refName, true)
  1010→	assert.NoError(t, err,
  1011→		"shadow branch should still exist after no-op turn end")
  1012→}
  1013→
  1014→// setupSessionWithCheckpoint initializes a session and creates one checkpoint
  1015→// on the shadow branch so there is content available for condensation.
  1016→func setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t *testing.T, s *ManualCommitStrategy, _ *git.Repository, dir, sessionID string) {
  1017→	t.Helper()
  1018→
  1019→	// Create metadata directory with a transcript file
  1020→	metadataDir := ".entire/metadata/" + sessionID
  1021→	metadataDirAbs := filepath.Join(dir, metadataDir)
  1022→	require.NoError(t, os.MkdirAll(metadataDirAbs, 0o755))
  1023→
  1024→	transcript := `{"type":"human","message":{"content":"test prompt"}}
  1025→{"type":"assistant","message":{"content":"test response"}}
  1026→`
  1027→	require.NoError(t, os.WriteFile(
  1028→		filepath.Join(metadataDirAbs, paths.TranscriptFileName),
  1029→		[]byte(transcript), 0o644))
  1030→
  1031→	// SaveChanges creates the shadow branch and checkpoint
  1032→	err := s.SaveChanges(SaveContext{
  1033→		SessionID:      sessionID,
  1034→		ModifiedFiles:  []string{},
  1035→		NewFiles:       []string{},
  1036→		DeletedFiles:   []string{},
  1037→		MetadataDir:    metadataDir,
  1038→		MetadataDirAbs: metadataDirAbs,
  1039→		CommitMessage:  "Checkpoint 1",
  1040→		AuthorName:     "Test",
  1041→		AuthorEmail:    "test@test.com",
  1042→	})
  1043→	require.NoError(t, err, "SaveChanges should succeed to create shadow branch content")
  1044→}
  1045→
  1046→// commitWithCheckpointTrailer creates a commit on the current branch with the
  1047→// Entire-Checkpoint trailer in the commit message. This simulates what happens
  1048→// after PrepareCommitMsg adds the trailer and the user completes the commit.
  1049→func commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t *testing.T, repo *git.Repository, dir, checkpointIDStr string) {
  1050→	t.Helper()
  1051→
  1052→	cpID := id.MustCheckpointID(checkpointIDStr)
  1053→
  1054→	// Modify a file so there is something to commit
  1055→	testFile := filepath.Join(dir, "test.txt")
  1056→	content := "updated at " + time.Now().String()
  1057→	require.NoError(t, os.WriteFile(testFile, []byte(content), 0o644))
  1058→
  1059→	wt, err := repo.Worktree()
  1060→	require.NoError(t, err)
  1061→
  1062→	_, err = wt.Add("test.txt")
  1063→	require.NoError(t, err)
  1064→
  1065→	commitMsg := "test commit\n\n" + trailers.CheckpointTrailerKey + ": " + cpID.String() + "\n"
  1066→	_, err = wt.Commit(commitMsg, &git.CommitOptions{
  1067→		Author: &object.Signature{
  1068→			Name:  "Test",
  1069→			Email: "test@test.com",
  1070→			When:  time.Now(),
  1071→		},
  1072→	})
  1073→	require.NoError(t, err, "commit with checkpoint trailer should succeed")
  1074→}
  1075→

     1→// Package agent provides interfaces and types for integrating with coding agents.
     2→// It abstracts agent-specific behavior (hooks, log parsing, session storage) so that
     3→// the same Strategy implementations can work with any coding agent.
     4→package agent
     5→
     6→import (
     7→	"io"
     8→)
     9→
    10→// Agent defines the interface for interacting with a coding agent.
    11→// Each agent implementation (Claude Code, Cursor, Aider, etc.) converts its
    12→// native format to the normalized types defined in this package.
    13→type Agent interface {
    14→	// Name returns the agent registry key (e.g., "claude-code", "gemini")
    15→	Name() AgentName
    16→
    17→	// Type returns the agent type identifier (e.g., "Claude Code", "Gemini CLI")
    18→	// This is stored in metadata and trailers.
    19→	Type() AgentType
    20→
    21→	// Description returns a human-readable description for UI
    22→	Description() string
    23→
    24→	// DetectPresence checks if this agent is configured in the repository
    25→	DetectPresence() (bool, error)
    26→
    27→	// GetHookConfigPath returns path to hook config file (empty if none)
    28→	GetHookConfigPath() string
    29→
    30→	// SupportsHooks returns true if agent supports lifecycle hooks
    31→	SupportsHooks() bool
    32→
    33→	// ParseHookInput parses hook callback input from stdin
    34→	ParseHookInput(hookType HookType, reader io.Reader) (*HookInput, error)
    35→
    36→	// GetSessionID extracts session ID from hook input
    37→	GetSessionID(input *HookInput) string
    38→
    39→	// TransformSessionID converts agent session ID to Entire session ID
    40→	TransformSessionID(agentSessionID string) string
    41→
    42→	// ExtractAgentSessionID extracts agent session ID from Entire ID
    43→	ExtractAgentSessionID(entireSessionID string) string
    44→
    45→	// GetSessionDir returns where agent stores session data for this repo.
    46→	// Examples:
    47→	//   Claude: ~/.claude/projects/<sanitized-repo-path>/
    48→	//   Aider: current working directory (returns repoPath)
    49→	//   Cursor: ~/Library/Application Support/Cursor/User/globalStorage/
    50→	GetSessionDir(repoPath string) (string, error)
    51→
    52→	// ReadSession reads session data from agent's storage.
    53→	// Handles different formats: JSONL (Claude), SQLite (Cursor), Markdown (Aider)
    54→	ReadSession(input *HookInput) (*AgentSession, error)
    55→
    56→	// WriteSession writes session data for resumption.
    57→	// Agent handles format conversion (JSONL, SQLite, etc.)
    58→	WriteSession(session *AgentSession) error
    59→
    60→	// FormatResumeCommand returns command to resume a session
    61→	FormatResumeCommand(sessionID string) string
    62→}
    63→
    64→// HookSupport is implemented by agents with lifecycle hooks.
    65→// This optional interface allows agents like Claude Code and Cursor to
    66→// install and manage hooks that notify Entire of agent events.
    67→type HookSupport interface {
    68→	Agent
    69→
    70→	// InstallHooks installs agent-specific hooks.
    71→	// If localDev is true, hooks point to local development build.
    72→	// If force is true, removes existing Entire hooks before installing.
    73→	// Returns the number of hooks installed.
    74→	InstallHooks(localDev bool, force bool) (int, error)
    75→
    76→	// UninstallHooks removes installed hooks
    77→	UninstallHooks() error
    78→
    79→	// AreHooksInstalled checks if hooks are currently installed
    80→	AreHooksInstalled() bool
    81→
    82→	// GetSupportedHooks returns the hook types this agent supports
    83→	GetSupportedHooks() []HookType
    84→}
    85→
    86→// HookHandler is implemented by agents that define their own hook vocabulary.
    87→// Each agent defines its own hook names (verbs) which become subcommands
    88→// under `entire hooks <agent>`. The actual handling is done by handlers
    89→// registered in the CLI package to avoid circular dependencies.
    90→//
    91→// This allows different agents to have completely different hook vocabularies
    92→// (e.g., Claude Code has "stop", Cursor might have "completion").
    93→type HookHandler interface {
    94→	Agent
    95→
    96→	// GetHookNames returns the hook verbs this agent supports.
    97→	// These are the subcommand names that will appear under `entire hooks <agent>`.
    98→	// e.g., ["stop", "user-prompt-submit", "pre-task", "post-task", "post-todo"]
    99→	GetHookNames() []string
   100→}
   101→
   102→// FileWatcher is implemented by agents that use file-based detection.
   103→// Agents like Aider that don't support hooks can use file watching
   104→// to detect session activity.
   105→type FileWatcher interface {
   106→	Agent
   107→
   108→	// GetWatchPaths returns paths to watch for session changes
   109→	GetWatchPaths() ([]string, error)
   110→
   111→	// OnFileChange handles a detected file change and returns session info
   112→	OnFileChange(path string) (*SessionChange, error)
   113→}
   114→
   115→// TranscriptAnalyzer is implemented by agents that support transcript analysis.
   116→// This allows agent-agnostic detection of work done between checkpoints.
   117→type TranscriptAnalyzer interface {
   118→	Agent
   119→
   120→	// GetTranscriptPosition returns the current position (length) of a transcript.
   121→	// For JSONL formats (Claude Code), this is the line count.
   122→	// For JSON formats (Gemini CLI), this is the message count.
   123→	// Returns 0 if the file doesn't exist or is empty.
   124→	// Use this to efficiently check if the transcript has grown since last checkpoint.
   125→	GetTranscriptPosition(path string) (int, error)
   126→
   127→	// ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset extracts files modified since a given offset.
   128→	// For JSONL formats (Claude Code), offset is the starting line number.
   129→	// For JSON formats (Gemini CLI), offset is the starting message index.
   130→	// Returns:
   131→	//   - files: list of file paths modified by the agent (from Write/Edit tools)
   132→	//   - currentPosition: the current position (line count or message count)
   133→	//   - error: any error encountered during reading
   134→	ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(path string, startOffset int) (files []string, currentPosition int, err error)
   135→}
   136→
   137→// TranscriptChunker is implemented by agents that support transcript chunking.
   138→// This allows agents to split large transcripts into chunks for storage (GitHub has
   139→// a 100MB blob limit) and reassemble them when reading.
   140→type TranscriptChunker interface {
   141→	Agent
   142→
   143→	// ChunkTranscript splits a transcript into chunks if it exceeds maxSize.
   144→	// Returns a slice of chunks. If the transcript fits in one chunk, returns single-element slice.
   145→	// The chunking is format-aware: JSONL splits at line boundaries, JSON splits message arrays.
   146→	ChunkTranscript(content []byte, maxSize int) ([][]byte, error)
   147→
   148→	// ReassembleTranscript combines chunks back into a single transcript.
   149→	// Handles format-specific reassembly (JSONL concatenation, JSON message merging).
   150→	ReassembleTranscript(chunks [][]byte) ([]byte, error)
   151→}
   152→

   429→// TranscriptChunker interface implementation
   430→
   431→// ChunkTranscript splits a JSONL transcript at line boundaries.
   432→// Claude Code uses JSONL format (one JSON object per line), so chunking
   433→// is done at newline boundaries to preserve message integrity.
   434→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ChunkTranscript(content []byte, maxSize int) ([][]byte, error) {
   435→	chunks, err := agent.ChunkJSONL(content, maxSize)
   436→	if err != nil {
   437→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to chunk JSONL transcript: %w", err)
   438→	}
   439→	return chunks, nil
   440→}
   441→
   442→// ReassembleTranscript concatenates JSONL chunks with newlines.
   443→//
   444→//nolint:unparam // error return is required by interface, kept for consistency
   445→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ReassembleTranscript(chunks [][]byte) ([]byte, error) {
   446→	return agent.ReassembleJSONL(chunks), nil
   447→}

head.Hash().String(),\n\t\tWorktreePath:                worktreePath,\n\t\tWorktreeID:                  worktreeID,\n\t\tStartedAt:                   now,\n\t\tPhase: session and save a checkpoint\n\tsetupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)\n\n\t// Set phase to ACTIVE\n\tstate, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\tstate.Phase = session.PhaseActive\n\trequire.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))\n\n\toriginalBaseCommit := state.BaseCommit\n\n\t// Create a commit WITHOUT a trailer (user removed it, or mid-turn commit\n\t// where PrepareCommitMsg couldn't add one due to Bug 1)\n\ttestFile := filepath.Join(dir, \"test.txt\")\n\trequire.NoError(t, os.WriteFile(testFile, []byte(\"no trailer commit\"), 0o644))\n\twt, err := repo.Worktree()\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\t_, err = wt.Add(\"test.txt\")\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\t_, err = wt.Commit(\"commit without trailer\", &git.CommitOptions{\n\t\tAuthor: &object.Signature{\n\t\t\tName:  \"Test\",\n\t\t\tEmail: \"test@test.com\",\n\t\t\tWhen:  time.Now(),\n\t\t},\n\t})\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\t// Get the new HEAD\n\thead, err := repo.Head()\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\tnewHeadHash := head.Hash().String()\n\trequire.NotEqual(t, originalBaseCommit, newHeadHash, \"HEAD should have changed\")\n\n\t// Run PostCommit\n\terr = s.PostCommit()\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\t// Verify BaseCommit was updated to new HEAD\n\tstate, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\tassert.Equal(t, newHeadHash, state.BaseCommit,\n\t\t\"BaseCommit should be updated to new HEAD even when commit has no trailer\")\n\n\t// Phase should stay ACTIVE (no state machine transition, just BaseCommit update)\n\tassert.Equal(t, session.PhaseActive, state.Phase,\n\t\t\"Phase should remain ACTIVE when commit has no trailer\")\n}\n\n// TestSaveChanges_PreservesPendingCheckpointID PendingCheckpointID (simulating what PostCommit does)\n\tstate, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\tstate.PendingCheckpointID = \"abc123def456\"\n\tstate.Phase = session.PhaseActiveCommitted\n\trequire.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))\n\n\t// Create metadata for a new checkpoint\n\tmetadataDir := \".entire/metadata/\" + sessionID\n\tmetadataDirAbs := should NOT clear PendingCheckpointID\n\terr = s.SaveChanges(SaveContext{\n\t\tSessionID:      sessionID,\n\t\tModifiedFiles:  []string{},\n\t\tNewFiles:       []string{\"src/new_file.go\"},\n\t\tDeletedFiles:   []string{},\n\t\tMetadataDir:    metadataDir,\n\t\tMetadataDirAbs: metadataDirAbs,\n\t\tCommitMessage:  \"Checkpoint 2\",\n\t\tAuthorName:     \"Test\",\n\t\tAuthorEmail:    \"test@test.com\",\n\t})\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\t// Reload state and verify PendingCheckpointID is preserved\n\tstate, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\tassert.Equal(t, \"abc123def456\", state.PendingCheckpointID,\n\t\t\"PendingCheckpointID should be preserved across SaveChanges calls, \"+\n\t\t\t\"not cleared — it's needed for deferred condensation at turn end\")\n}\n"
